### [1] [How Much Does It Cost to Answer My Question? Benchmarking Cloud VLM-based VQA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07861)
*Henri Vanhuynegem,Weitao Xu,Yiran Shen,Guohao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VQABench是首个将客户端输入预处理作为可控变量、系统评估云VLM推理的移动VQA系统的基准测试。它评估了12种预处理技术在3个VQA数据集、4个商业VLM（来自3家提供商）上的表现，共95,168次API调用，揭示了预处理并非普遍有益，其效果取决于目标模型、API范式、提供商token计费规则和任务形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上的VQA系统越来越多地将推理任务卸载到云端VLM，但专有API对模型内部和服务行为控制有限，客户端预处理成为下游开发者主要的优化空间。尽管已有大量预处理技术被提出，但其对商业云VLM的成本-质量影响从未被系统研究过。

Method: 构建VQABench基准框架，将客户端输入预处理作为受控变量，评估12种预处理技术，覆盖3个VQA数据集和4个来自3家提供商的商业VLM，通过95,168次API调用系统测量回答质量、负载大小、token成本和系统延迟等多维度指标。

Result: 预处理并非普遍有效：其效果高度依赖于目标模型、API范式、提供商的token计费规则以及任务形式。不恰当的预处理策略可能增加部署成本或延迟，同时降低回答准确率。

Conclusion: VQABench明确了预处理何时有效、何时失效及其原因，为未来研究方向和移动VQA系统的实际部署提供了指导性洞见，填补了商业云VLM场景下客户端预处理成本-质量评估的空白。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are becoming a practical backend for mobile visual question answering (VQA) systems, enabling smartphones and smart glasses to answer users' questions about the physical world. Since modern VLMs remain difficult to run on mobile and edge devices, VQA systems increasingly offload inference to cloud-based VLMs. This gives mobile devices access to stronger computation, but it also makes visual input preparation a key system variable: how the image is prepared before offloading affects not only answer quality but also payload size, token cost, and system latency. Proprietary APIs expose little control over model internals or serving behavior, leaving client-side preprocessing as the main practical optimization space for downstream developers. Many such techniques have been proposed for visual offloading, yet their cost-quality impact on commercial cloud VLMs has never been studied. To fill this gap, we present VQABench, the first systematic benchmark that treats client-side input preprocessing as a controlled variable for cloud-VLM-based VQA. We evaluate 12 preprocessing techniques across three VQA datasets and four commercial VLMs from three providers, totaling 95,168 API calls. Our results show that preprocessing is not universally beneficial: its effectiveness depends on the target model, API paradigm, provider token-accounting rule, and task formulation. A poorly selected preprocessing strategy can increase deployment cost or latency while degrading answer accuracy. Overall, our benchmark clarifies when preprocessing helps, when it fails, and why, providing insights to guide future research and real-world deployment of VQA systems.

</details>


### [2] [SCoPE: Training-Free Audio-Visual Event Perception via Sparse Cross-Modal Prior Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07923)
*Jaemo Jeong,Junho Yoon,Hyunju Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoPE，一种无需训练的视听事件感知框架，通过让所有查询标签竞争共享证据、模态间互相引导事件选择的方式，消除错误标签的"假共激活"（FCA）问题。在LLP数据集上，使用冻结的CLIP+CLAP骨干，SCoPE相比AV²A在Type@seg上提升7.45点、Event@seg上提升5.04点，且无需修改配置可直接迁移到OV-AVEBench和VGGSound-AVEL100k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无需训练方法通过匹配冻结的音频/视觉特征与文本编码的事件名称来查询新事件词汇，但相关标签共享证据，导致错误标签可能得分不低于正确标签（即假共激活问题）。不存在标量阈值能在拒绝错误标签的同时保留所有正确标签，类特定阈值虽可防止错误标签成为最终预测，但FCA仍残留在底层得分向量中。

Method: SCoPE是一个无需训练的框架，核心机制包括：(1) 所有被查询的标签竞争共享证据；(2) 每个模态指导另一模态的事件选择。作者还推导了在双标签拟合中竞争消除FCA的精确条件。

Result: 在LLP数据集上使用相同的冻结CLIP+CLAP骨干，SCoPE在Type@seg上比已报告的AV²A值提升7.45点，在Event@seg上提升5.04点。同一固定配置无需修改即可迁移到OV-AVEBench和VGGSound-AVEL100k数据集。

Conclusion: 通过标签间竞争共享证据与跨模态互导机制，SCoPE能够从根源上消除假共激活问题，在多个基准上显著提升视听事件感知性能，且具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-visual event perception (AVEP) determines which events occur in a video, when they occur, and whether they are audible, visible, or both. Training-free methods query new event vocabularies by matching frozen audio and visual features with text-encoded event names. However, related labels share evidence. An incorrect label can then score at least as high as a correct one. We call this a false co-activation (FCA). No scalar cutoff can reject the incorrect label while keeping every correct one. Class-specific thresholds may prevent that label from becoming a final prediction, but the FCA remains in the underlying score vector. We introduce SCoPE, a training-free framework in which all queried labels compete for shared evidence and each modality guides event selection in the other. We derive an exact condition for when this competition removes an FCA in a two-label fit. With identical frozen CLIP+CLAP backbones on LLP, SCoPE improves Type@seg by 7.45 points and Event@seg by 5.04 points compared with the reported AV$^2$A values. The same fixed configuration transfers unchanged to OV-AVEBench and VGGSound-AVEL100k.

</details>


### [3] [PragyaDoc: A Universal Document Intelligence Framework for Multilingual Medical Document Understanding in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07478)
*Jagpal Singh Jhala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PragyaDoc，一个面向印度多种官方语言的通用文档智能框架，通过四层流水线（并行集成OCR、几何词法融合、确定性领域结构化、双LLM医学推理与本地化）将英文医学文档转化为可被农村患者、ASHA工作者和患者家属理解的本地语言内容，解决医疗信息可及性严重失衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拥有22种官方语言，但绝大多数医学文献仅以英文存在，而最迫切需要这些信息的群体（农村人口、ASHA工作者、患者家属）被功能性排除在理解之外，形成了严重的医疗信息可及性障碍。

Method: 提出PragyaDoc四层流水线框架：1）并行集成OCR提取层；2）几何-词法融合层；3）确定性领域结构化层；4）双LLM医学推理与本地化层。

Result: 本文为摘要，未提供具体的定量实验结果。

Conclusion: PragyaDoc作为通用文档智能框架，通过多层次的OCR提取、融合、结构化与本地化处理，有望弥合印度多语言环境下医学文档的可及性鸿沟。

Abstract: India's 22 official languages create a critical accessibility barrier: the majority of medical documentation exists exclusively in English, yet the patients who most urgently require this information - rural populations, ASHA workers, and patient families - are functionally excluded from understanding it. This paper presents PragyaDoc, a Universal Document Intelligence Framework that addresses this gap through a four-layer pipeline: a parallel ensemble OCR extraction layer, a geometric-lexical fusion layer, a deterministic domain structuring layer, and a dual-LLM medical reasoning and localization layer

</details>


### [4] [Your VLM Already Knows When: Training-Free Temporal Grounding by Asking Yes or No](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08315)
*Ji Huang,Barry Devereux,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型（VLM）在识别事件内容上表现良好，但在预测事件发生时间上严重失败（如Charades-STA上R@0.5仅3.8%，且模型"自信地犯错"，熵检测失效）。作者证明失败根源在于任务接口设计而非感知能力：固定权重不变，将时间戳回归替换为粗到细的二值问题扫描（仅利用首token概率进行排序），可在四个冻结骨干网络上将R@0.5提升28-50个点。残差失败分解为感知轴（随骨干变化）和几何轴（可由输出窗口与事件宽度之比解析预测）两个维度。基于此分析提出的无训练方法FV-Action在Charades-STA上达到56.8% R@0.5，超越同骨干的固有接地流水线及最强无训练结果，并在TACoS、ActivityNet Captions和QVHighlights上均优于现有方法，全程无需任何时间监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM虽然在事件识别任务上表现可靠，但在预测事件发生时间（时间戳定位）上严重失败，且模型表现出"自信的错误"——熵检测无法帮助识别这些错误。作者希望探究这个失败的根本原因，并找到一种无需训练即可大幅提升时间定位能力的方法。

Method: 作者提出假设：时间定位失败源于任务接口设计而非感知能力。具体方法是将时间戳回归替换为粗到细的二值问题扫描（coarse-to-fine scan of binary questions），仅使用模型首token概率作为排序信号而不作为绝对置信度。在此基础上，作者将残差失败分解为感知轴（perception axis，随骨干网络变化）和几何轴（geometry axis，可解析预测）。基于此分析构建无训练方法FV-Action。

Result: ① 在四个冻结骨干网络上，粗到细二值扫描将R@0.5提升28-50个点；② FV-Action在Charades-STA达到56.8% R@0.5，超越同骨干的固有接地流水线和最强无训练方法；③ 在TACoS上超越所有TVG训练模型零样本评估结果；④ 在ActivityNet Captions和QVHighlights上优于直接预测方法。

Conclusion: 时间定位失败是接口设计问题而非感知缺陷，通过粗到细二值问题扫描+排序策略可显著改善VLMs的时间定位能力。残差失败可分解为可测量的感知轴和解析可预测的几何轴，为该任务提供了明确的分析框架。FV-Action证明了无需任何时间监督即可实现SOTA级无训练时间定位。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs that recognise events reliably still fail to say when they happen. Prompted for timestamps, strong VLMs reach as little as $3.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, and $77$ to $80\%$ of their wrong predictions carry low output entropy: the models are confidently wrong, and entropy-based error detection stays below a random classifier. We show that this failure lives in the task interface, not in perception. Holding the weights fixed, replacing timestamp regression with a coarse-to-fine scan of binary questions, whose first-token probabilities are consumed only as a ranking, raises R@0.5 by $28$ to $50$ points across four frozen backbones. The residual failures decompose into two measurable axes: a perception axis that moves with the backbone, and a geometry axis that is analytically predictable from the ratio of the output-window and event widths. FV-Action, the training-free method built on this analysis, reaches $56.8\%$ R@0.5 on Charades-STA, above the same backbone's native grounding pipeline and the strongest training-free result on this benchmark; it surpasses every TVG-trained model evaluated zero-shot on TACoS, and improves over direct prediction on ActivityNet Captions and QVHighlights, with no temporal supervision at any stage.

</details>


### [5] [NeuroPilot: An Agent-Driven Smart Pipeline for Processing, Quality Control, and Managing Neuroimages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07541)
*Yiyao Chen,Yucheng Li,Jungong Tong,Shaoqi Wang,Kunhao Zhou,Ziquan Wei,Monica Murea,Marissa DiPiero,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroPilot是一个基于LLM的多智能体系统，将神经影像数据处理过程中的数据标准化、预处理和质量控制三个阶段数字化为三个可调用的技能模块（dcm2bids-skill、neuroimage-pre-skill、qc-agent-skill），实现全自动工作流编排。该系统已在17个队列（超过12.3万受试者）中部署验证，覆盖从婴儿到老年人群及多种MRI模态，将传统2-3个月的训练和处理周期压缩至一周。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像原始数据转换为可分析衍生数据依赖数据标准化、预处理和质量控制三个阶段，但现有工具虽各自成熟，其编排却需要项目专属脚本、环境适应性调参和大量人工QC，缺乏自动化、可扩展的端到端解决方案。

Method: NeuroPilot将神经影像处理、QC和数据管理专家的经验数字化为三个LLM可调用的技能模块，由LLM驱动智能体自主编排处理流程，将不同基础设施环境统一为单一配置以实现最大扩展性。系统按模态和队列特征动态路由数据至最优预处理管线，并通过多层级验证系统驱动的半自动3D浏览器仪表盘QC机制优化失败案例。

Result: QC智能体筛查了558个生产受试者，自动标记结果与FreeSurfer拓扑缺陷指标验证一致；婴儿处理管线在QC验证输入上实现100%（201/201）完成率；系统在17个队列（超过12.3万受试者）中成功部署，覆盖婴儿至老年人群及结构、弥散、功能多种MRI模态。

Conclusion: NeuroPilot通过多智能体架构显著提升了神经影像数据处理的自动化程度、可扩展性和效率，将传统2-3个月的训练与完整数据集处理时间压缩至一周，展示了LLM驱动自动编排在神经影像领域的强大潜力与实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Transforming raw neuroimage archives into analysis-ready derivatives relies on three brittle stages: data standardization, modality-specific preprocessing, and quality control (QC). While individual neuroimaging tools are well developed, their orchestration requires project-specific scripts, environment-adaptive tuning, and labor-intensive manual QC. To address this, we introduce NeuroPilot, a multi-agent system that digitalizes the expertise of neuroimage processing, QC, and data management into three LLM-invocable skills: dcm2bids-skill, neuroimage-pre-skill, and qc-agent-skill. The LLM-driven agent autonomously orchestrates workflows, generalizing various infrastructure settings into a single configuration to achieve the highest scalability. Demonstrating the system's generalizability, we deployed NeuroPilot across 17 cohorts (>123,000 subjects) spanning infant to aging populations and multiple MRI modalities (structural, diffusion, functional). In practice, after standardizing data via the dcm2bids-skill, the agent dynamically routes datasets to the optimal neuroimage-pre-skill based on available modalities and cohort traits (e.g., dispatching T1w and fMRI data to fMRIPrep, or selecting specialized pipelines for infant cohorts). The qc-agent-skill then drives an evidence-based, semi-automated QC via a 3-D browser dashboard, utilizing a multi-tiered verification system to optimize failed cases and escalate complex issues for supervisor inspection. Quantitatively, our QC agent screened 558 production subjects, validating its automated flags against FreeSurfer's topology-defect metrics. The infant processing pipeline achieved a 100% (201/201) completion rate on QC-validated inputs. Importantly, NeuroPilot compresses the traditional 2--3 month timeline for training staff and processing complete datasets into a single week. NeuroPilot is deployed in https://wanda-cyberbench.com/.

</details>


### [6] [MotionCraft: Latent World Modeling with Sparse Attention for Visual Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08553)
*Rong Fu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Xiaowen Ma,Yongtai Liu,Wangyu Wu,Shuo Yin,Zijian Zhang,Sicheng Li,Yingrui Ji,Chenhao Wang,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCraft是一个将视频超分辨率重建表述为基于世界模型的运动感知潜状态预测的可控框架，结合自适应稀疏注意力与用户可控接口，在流式约束下实现时间一致且高质量的重建，并支持时间平滑度与重建保真度之间的可预测权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VSR方法在局部细节保真、长程时空建模、感知真实性与效率之间存在难以调和的权衡：卷积对齐方法在大运动或复杂退化下表现不佳，Transformer方法计算代价高，潜空间或扩散生成方法需要额外的时间一致性约束。作者希望提出一个同时兼顾重建质量、时间一致性、可控性与流式效率的统一框架。

Method: MotionCraft包含三个核心组件：(1) 鲁棒运动融合(Robust Motion Fusion)，用于处理大运动与复杂退化下的对齐问题；(2) 潜世界变换器(Latent World Transformer)，借鉴世界模型的理念将恢复问题建模为运动感知的潜状态预测，通过自适应稀疏注意力在局部性与目标非局部交互之间取得平衡；(3) 紧凑条件解码器(Compact Conditional Decoder)，在流式约束下生成时间一致的重建结果。此外框架集成了显式的用户可访问控制接口，允许调整时间平滑度与重建保真度之间的权衡。

Result: 经验评估表明MotionCraft取得了强竞争力的重建与感知性能，同时能够在时间平滑度与重建保真度之间提供可预测的权衡调节能力，满足流式应用场景的需求。

Conclusion: MotionCraft通过将视频超分辨率重新定义为运动感知的潜状态预测问题，并整合自适应稀疏注意力与可控接口，有效缓解了传统VSR方法在质量、效率与可控性之间的冲突，为流式视频恢复任务提供了一种兼具性能与灵活性的新方案。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) aims to recover high-fidelity high-resolution videos from low-resolution inputs and is central to applications ranging from mobile capture to streaming and archival restoration. Existing approaches trade off among local-detail fidelity, long-range spatio-temporal modeling, perceptual realism, and efficiency: convolutional alignment techniques preserve local structure but suffer when motion is large or degradations are complex; transformer-based methods capture long-range dependencies yet require architectural or algorithmic adaptations to remain computationally feasible; and recent latent or diffusion-based generators synthesize rich texture but require specialized temporal constraints to maintain coherence. We present MotionCraft, a controllable VSR framework that formulates restoration as motion-aware latent state prediction inspired by world models and integrates adaptive sparse attention with an explicit user-accessible control interface. MotionCraft combines robust motion fusion, a Latent World Transformer that balances locality and targeted non-local interactions, and a compact conditional decoder to deliver temporally consistent, high-quality reconstructions under streaming constraints. Empirical evaluations show that MotionCraft achieves strong reconstruction and perceptual performance while enabling predictable trade-offs between temporal smoothness and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [7] [Performance of large language models in the optical diagnosis of colorectal polyps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07543)
*Joshua C. Vences,William T. Tran,Nikko Gimpaya,Catharine M. Walsh,Rishad J. Khan,Robert Bechara,Asher C. Wiggins,Celine N. Rousan,Kaitlyn V. G. L. Morgado,Angie Ibrahim,Kevin H. M. Kuo,Daniel von Renteln,Alexander Hann,Dennis L. Shung,Michael A. Scaffidi,Charles Ménard,Joshua Landy,Samir C. Grover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用PRIME数据集（132例白光和窄带成像图像）评估了五种多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在结直肠息肉分类和组织学预测方面的诊断准确性。结果显示所有模型在区分肿瘤性与非肿瘤性息肉方面的F1分数均>0.9，其中Gemini 2.5 Pro在区分浸润性与非浸润性息肉及低/高级别腺瘤方面表现最佳，而Claude Opus 4和GPT-5在巴黎分类中正确率最高（41.7%），但灵敏度和特异性均未达到ESGE标准，作者强调需进行前瞻性多中心试验并设计人机协同工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的光学诊断可指导结直肠息肉的手术切除策略和随访方案，多模态大语言模型有望用于基于图像的息肉诊断，但缺乏对这些模型诊断准确性的系统评估，因此需要全面比较不同MLLM在结直肠息肉分类和组织学预测方面的表现。

Method: 这是一项回顾性诊断性能研究，使用PRIME数据集（包含白光和NBI图像）评估五种MLLM（Claude Opus 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-o3、GPT-4o和GPT-5）。针对132例病例，使用F1分数、正确率百分比和准确率来评估各模型在巴黎分类、NICE分类和预测组织学中的表现，并以专家应答为金标准，采用Cochran's Q检验和McNemar检验确定模型间预测差异。

Result: 所有模型在区分肿瘤性vs.非肿瘤性息肉方面的F1分数均超过0.9。Gemini 2.5 Pro在区分浸润性vs.非浸润性息肉及低vs.高级别腺瘤方面表现最佳，F1分数分别为0.560和0.492。Claude Opus 4和GPT-5在巴黎分类中正确率最高（41.7%），且统计上显著高于其他模型。但所有模型的灵敏度和特异性均未达到ESGE标准。

Conclusion: Claude Opus 4和Gemini 2.5 Pro在区分息肉亚型方面表现最佳，最接近专家共识水平。但考虑到灵敏度和特异性未能满足ESGE临床标准，建议在临床部署前进行前瞻性多中心试验，并设计人机协同（human-in-the-loop）工作流程。

Abstract: Background and Study Aims: Accurate optical diagnosis of colorectal polyps guides resection strategy and surveillance, with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) showing potential for image-based diagnosis. We aimed to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of MLLMs in classifying colorectal polyps and predicting histology. Methods: We conducted a retrospective diagnostic performance study using the PRIME dataset, a curated set of white light and narrow-band imaging (NBI) images. We evaluated Claude Opus 4, Google Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT-o3, GPT-4o, and GPT-5. For Paris, Narrow-band Imaging Colorectal Endoscopic (NICE), and predicted histology, we calculated F1 scores, percent correct scores, and accuracy of each MLLM compared to expert responses for 132 cases. Cochran's Q and McNemar's Test were used to determine differences between predicted values of each MLLM. Results: The F1 scores among MLLMs were >0.9 for all models for neoplastic vs. non-neoplastic polyps. Gemini 2.5 Pro demonstrated the highest F1 scores for invasive vs. non-invasive polyps and low- vs. high-grade adenoma, at 0.560 and 0.492 respectively. Claude Opus 4 and GPT-5 had statistically significantly higher percent correct scores than other MLLMs at 41.7%, using Paris classification. Conclusions: Claude Opus 4 and Gemini 2.5 Pro showed the highest accuracy in differentiating polyp subtypes, performing closest to expert consensus. Sensitivity and specificity, however, did not meet ESGE standards, highlighting the need for prospective multicenter trials and the design of human-in-the-loop workflows before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [8] [GRASP: Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype Learning for Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Understanding in Drone Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09270)
*Jiahui Cui,Yan Zhao,Kan Wei,Enze Zhu,Peirong Zhang,Lei Wang,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP学习框架，通过区域聚焦对齐和语义原型两种协同策略，解决无人机航拍视角下视觉-语言导航中的跨模态焦点错位和视觉同构两大挑战，在GeoText-1652基准和ERA数据集上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍场景的宽视野和俯视视角给视觉-语言理解带来双重挑战：宏观上背景杂乱导致跨模态焦点错位（模型关注全局环境相似性而非具体目标细节），微观上视觉同构造成歧义（候选对象几何结构相似但属性细微不同）。现有方法难以有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出GRASP（Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype）学习框架，包含两个协同策略：（1）区域聚焦对齐（RFA）促进以对象为中心的跨模态对齐并抑制背景干扰；（2）语义扰动增强匹配（SPEM）利用前景纯化的语义原型码本（SPC）构建语义扰动负样本，用于细粒度语义判别。

Result: 在GeoText-1652基准和未见过的ERA数据集上的大量实验表明，GRASP在无人机航拍视图细粒度图文检索中取得竞争性性能，验证了其在航空场景跨模态理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRASP框架通过区域级对齐和语义原型扰动匹配有效地解决了无人机视角下的细粒度跨模态理解难题，为航空视觉语言导航提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-modal understanding in drone views is essential for aerial vision-language navigation. However, the inherent wide field of view and overhead perspective of drone scenarios impose dual challenges on vision-language understanding. At the macro level, overwhelming background clutter in visual representations leads to Cross-Modal Focus Misalignment, where the model prioritizes global environmental similarities over specific object details. At the micro level, Visual Isomorphism creates ambiguity, where candidates share similar geometric structures yet differ only in subtle attributes. To address these challenges, we propose the Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype (GRASP) learning framework, enhancing discriminative capability through two synergistic strategies. Specifically, we introduce Region-Focused Alignment (RFA) to promote object-centric cross-modal alignment while suppressing background interference. Concurrently, to tackle visual isomorphism, we propose Semantic Perturbation Enhanced Matching (SPEM), which leverages a foreground-purified Semantic Prototype Codebook (SPC) to construct semantically perturbed negatives for fine-grained semantic discrimination. Extensive experiments on the GeoText-1652 benchmark and the unseen ERA dataset demonstrate that GRASP achieves competitive performance in drone-view fine-grained image-text retrieval, validating its effectiveness for cross-modal understanding in aerial scenarios. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/UCAS-JC/GRASP.

</details>


### [9] [Learning an Interior Layout Policy in a Domain Specific Language Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07547)
*Yuhao Lu,Weichen Zhang,Wenyi Xiao,Haohui Chen,Yiyun Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LayoutDSL，一种基于LLM的室内场景布局生成框架，通过在领域特定语言（DSL）动作空间中学习布局策略，将布局推理从连续坐标回归转化为结构化符号动作决策，并结合3D-FrontDSL数据集和基于设计原则的强化学习奖励机制，显著提升空间合理性与设计逻辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景布局生成方法存在两方面根本缺陷：一是将房间条件简化为粗略的3D边界框，忽略了门窗等结构要素；二是将空间推理表述为直接的坐标预测，即把布局设计当作连续几何参数回归问题，阻碍了模型学习智能布局设计背后的推理逻辑。

Method: LayoutDSL采用LLM-based框架，在DSL动作空间中学习室内布局策略。核心包括：(1) DSL提供布局信息的显式符号表示，作为结构化动作空间，每个动作对应一个可解释的设计决策；(2) 构建3D-FrontDSL数据集，包含房间结构注释与合成的DSL动作序列用于监督微调；(3) 设计基于室内设计原则和物理合理性的奖励函数，通过强化学习优化策略，以获得更可泛化、可扩展且可验证的策略。

Result: 大量实验表明，LayoutDSL在空间合理性和设计逻辑性方面较现有强基线方法有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将布局生成从连续坐标回归范式转变为DSL符号动作空间中的策略学习范式是有效的，该方法能够更好地捕捉布局设计的内在推理逻辑，并实现可验证反馈下的泛化学习。

Abstract: Indoor scene layout generation is a challenging task in interior design. Existing methods often oversimplify the task by reducing room conditions to coarse 3D bounding boxes and neglecting structural elements such as doors and windows. More fundamentally, many prior approaches formulate spatial reasoning as direct coordinate prediction, thereby casting interior layout design as continuous regression over raw geometric parameters, which hinders the model from learning the underlying reasoning logic of intelligent layout design. We propose \textbf{LayoutDSL}, a novel LLM-based framework for learning an interior layout policy in a domain-specific language (DSL) action space. The DSL provides an explicit symbolic representation of layout information and serves as a structured action space for layout reasoning, where each action corresponds to an interpretable design decision. Under this DSL-based policy learning paradigm, we construct 3D-FrontDSL, a dataset of room-structure annotations paired with synthetic DSL action sequences for supervised fine-tuning. To promote a more generalizable and scalable policy with verifiable feedback, we design rewards grounded in interior design principles and physical plausibility, and optimize the policy via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LayoutDSL substantially improves spatial plausibility and design logicality over strong baselines and existing methods.

</details>


### [10] [P2Voxel: Pyramid Pivot Voxelization for 3D Mesh Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07549)
*Zhenhong Sun,Haozhe Liu,Yifu Wang,Xibin Song,Senbo Wang,Huadong Mo,Daoyi Dong,Hongdong Li,Pan Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P2Voxel，一个金字塔枢轴体素化框架，用于紧凑且重建感知的3D网格token化。通过局部平面性、空间复杂度和块可重建性三个假设，将三角网格转化为紧凑、结构化的金字塔枢轴token，实现高效的下游3D网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角网格虽然能提供显式且精确的表面几何，但其不规则的拓扑连接性使得3D网格token化成为一个几何采样问题。传统方法依赖场中心体积采样或边交点表面采样，未能充分利用局部几何证据。本文希望识别每个活跃体素内最少的几何证据，以支持确定性的表面恢复。

Method: P2Voxel框架包含三个关键创新：(1) 在局部平面性假设下，Pivot体素化用表面枢轴和方向符号表示每个活跃体素，最小化局部证据同时保证确定性重建；(2) 在空间复杂度假设下，金字塔Pivot体素化根据表面几何复杂度分配不同密度的token；(3) 在块可重建性假设下，金字塔VAE学习紧凑的多分辨率潜码，避免为整个高分辨率体素形状建模密集全局场。

Result: 通过上述设计，P2Voxel能够将网格转换为紧凑、结构化的可学习金字塔枢轴token，实现下游3D任务中的高效网格重建。

Conclusion: P2Voxel通过重新定义网格token化为局部表面证据采样，结合金字塔多分辨率结构和VAE潜空间学习，为3D网格表示提供了一种紧凑、可学习且重建感知的token化方案。

Abstract: Triangle meshes provide explicit and accurate surface geometry, yet their irregular topology connectivity makes 3D mesh tokenization a geometric sampling problem: how to sample and organize geometric evidence into compact, structured and learnable tokens. Beyond field-centric volumetric sampling and edge-intersection surface sampling, we retarget mesh tokenization as \textit{local surface evidence sampling}: identifying the minimal geometric evidence inside each active voxel that is sufficient for deterministic surface recovery. To this end, we introduce \textbf{P2Voxel}, a pyramid pivot voxelization framework for compact and reconstruction-aware mesh tokenization. P2Voxel is built on three key innovations. Under the \textit{Local Planarity} assumption, Pivot Voxelization represents each active voxel with a surface pivot and an orientation sign, providing minimal local evidence that can induce the corner values required for deterministic reconstruction. Under the \textit{Spatial Complexity} assumption, Pyramid Pivot Voxelization exploits the spatial non-uniformity of real surfaces by allocating finer pivot tokens to geometrically complex regions while keeping smooth regions coarse and compact. Under the \textit{Block Reconstructability} assumption, a Pyramid VAE learns compact multi-resolution latent codes over locally reconstructable pivot blocks, avoiding the need to model the entire high-resolution voxelized shape as a dense global field. Together, these designs convert meshes into compact, structured, and learnable pyramid pivot tokens, enabling efficient mesh reconstruction for downstream 3D tasks.

</details>


### [11] [Auditing Medical Vision-Language Models on Chest Radiographs: Estimating Reference Agreement Across Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07550)
*Pengyang Yu,Yiou Wang,Zhongping Dong,Sahraoui Dhelim,Chun-Mei Feng,M. Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个生成式视觉语言模型在三个机构胸部X光片语料库、六种发现、两种提示协议下超过34.5万条发现级预测中的参考标准一致性，发现跨机构、跨发现、跨预测方向的参考一致性转移不可靠，且自适应选择七个估计器并未优于简单固定备选方案，结论是参考一致性需按站点和接口逐案重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在返回结构化胸部X光片发现时不暴露置信度，接收机构无法判断单个判断的可信程度。参考标准一致性是否能在不同站点、发现类型、预测方向和问题格式间迁移，很大程度上尚未被测量，这构成了评估模型可信度与机构间可迁移性的核心缺口。

Method: 在三个机构胸部X光片语料库上评估三个生成式视觉语言模型，覆盖六种发现类型和两种提示协议，生成超过34.5万条发现级预测。用一个小的本地标注预算估计接收机构的逐发现-方向参考一致性，并在严格的机构留出重复评估下对七种估计策略进行压力测试，同时比较不同估计器及基于后验预测的计数区间覆盖率。

Result: 在完全排除接收机构参与开发的情况下，自适应选择七个估计器未优于固定替代方案：自适应选择平均Brier分数为0.1083，而始终使用Beta-Binomial经验贝叶斯估计器为0.0853，仅用目标的逻辑模型为0.0855。后两者差异仅0.0003，小于该族估计器对求解器版本变化的敏感性，且各自在约半数场景中占优，因此无法推荐默认方案。跨机构池化估计器的优势集中在一个站点，按机构聚类后不具确证性；名义95%的plug-in经验贝叶斯后验预测计数区间实际覆盖率为87.0%，在最难的机构更低。

Conclusion: 参考标准一致性必须按站点和接口逐案重新评估，不能跨机构、发现类型或方向默认迁移。研究结果仅涉及与机构标签的一致性，不代表临床正确性，且当前不存在可普遍推荐的默认估计器。

Abstract: Vision-language models return structured chest-radiograph findings through interfaces exposing no confidence score, so a receiving institution cannot read off how far to trust an individual judgment. Whether agreement with an institution's reference standard transfers across sites, findings, prediction directions and question formats is largely unmeasured. We evaluated three generative vision-language models on three institutional chest-radiograph corpora and six findings under two elicitation protocols, comprising more than 345,000 finding-level predictions, and estimated finding-by-direction reference agreement at a receiving institution from a small budget of local labels. Estimation strategies were then stress-tested under repeated strict institution-held-out evaluation. Under evaluation excluding the receiving institution from development entirely, adaptive selection among the seven estimators that design admits did not improve on simple fixed alternatives: it achieved a mean Brier score of 0.1083, against 0.0853 for always using a Beta-Binomial empirical-Bayes estimator and 0.0855 for a target-only logistic model. Those two differ by 0.0003, less than this family's own sensitivity to a change of solver version, and each leads in about half the settings, so no default can be recommended. Their advantage over estimators pooling across institutions was concentrated at one site and not confirmatory once clustered by institution, and a plug-in empirical-Bayes posterior-predictive count interval at a nominal 95% level covered 87.0%, less at the hardest institution. Reference agreement therefore has to be re-evaluated per site and per interface; these results concern agreement with institutional labels, not clinical correctness.

</details>


### [12] [Impact of Dataset Composition on Embedded Real-Time UAV Wildfire Detection Using Compact YOLO Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07554)
*Eduardo de los Santos,Andre S. Kelbouscas,Ricardo B. Grando,Bruna V. Guterres*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对无人机视觉火灾检测中真实训练图像稀缺的问题，以紧凑型YOLO模型作为受控验证家族，评估了四种训练配置（真实未增强、真实增强、混合未增强、混合增强）对嵌入式实时火灾检测性能的影响。结果表明，真实未增强数据集获得了最佳的召回率与平均精度平衡，合成数据混合和图像增强均未能产生更优的部署选择，说明数据集真实性与领域对齐比通过合成扩展数据集规模更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉火灾检测系统受限于缺乏多样化的真实世界训练图像，本文旨在探究数据集构成（真实图像与AI生成合成图像的混合、以及图像增强策略）对嵌入式实时无人机火灾检测性能的实际影响，以确定在资源受限部署条件下最优的数据集配置策略。

Method: 以紧凑型YOLO模型作为受控验证家族，设计四种训练配置进行对比实验：真实非增强、真实增强、混合非增强（真实+AI合成图像）和混合增强。通过评估各配置在实际检测性能（召回率与平均精度）上的表现来确定最佳训练数据集方案。

Result: 实验结果显示，真实非增强数据集获得了最佳整体工作点，在召回率与平均精度之间取得最强平衡；合成数据混合与图像增强均未产生更好的最终部署选择。

Conclusion: 对于嵌入式无人机火灾检测，数据集真实性与领域对齐的价值高于通过合成扩展增加训练集规模，建议在实际部署中优先保障训练数据的真实性与领域匹配度。

Abstract: The development of vision-based wildfire detection systems for unmanned aerial vehicles is constrained by the limited availability of diverse real-world training images. This paper investigates the impact of dataset composition on embedded real-time UAV wildfire detection using compact YOLO models as a controlled validation family. Four training configurations were evaluated: real non-augmented, real augmented, hybrid non-augmented, and hybrid augmented, where the hybrid sets combine real wildfire images with AI-generated samples. The objective is to determine whether synthetic data mixing and image augmentation improve practical detection performance under resource-constrained deployment conditions. Experimental results show that the best overall operating point was obtained with the real non-augmented dataset, which achieved the strongest balance between recall and mean average precision for UAV-based wildfire detection. The results also show that neither hybridization with synthetic data nor augmentation produced a better final deployment choice. These findings suggest that, for embedded UAV wildfire detection, dataset realism and domain alignment are more valuable than increasing training set size through synthetic expansion.

</details>


### [13] [MVMD: A Multi-View Approach for Enhanced Mirror Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07559)
*Yidan Shen,Yu Wen,Chen Zhang,Xin Fu,Renjie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVMD，一种多视角镜子检测方法，并构建了首个多视角镜子检测数据集。该方法利用跨视角和自注意力机制，通过三个关键模块（Inter-Views Block、Intra-View Block、Refinement Block）分别处理视角变化下镜子内物体的位移、镜子内反射检测以及边界细化，相比单图像镜子检测方法在准确率上提升2.6%，IoU提升11.1%，显著改善三维重建在镜子密集环境中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维重建中，镜子会引入畸变和碎片化空间，导致重建模型不准确不可靠。传统方法仅依赖单图像进行镜子检测，忽略了多视角设置中蕴含的丰富信息。而三维重建本质上依赖多视角图像，因此在重建前利用多视角图像进行镜子检测可以更有效地解决问题。

Method: 提出的MVMD方法基于物体在不同视角下、以及镜子内外反射之间的固有关联，通过跨注意力（cross-attention）和自注意力（self-attention）机制来学习这些关系。模型包含三个关键模块：Inter-Views Block用于追踪视角变化导致的镜子内物体位移；Intra-View Block用于检测镜子内的物体反射；Refinement Block用于细化镜子边界并增强检测细节。

Result: 实验结果表明，与单图像镜子检测方法相比，该方法在准确率上提升最高达2.6%，在IoU上提升最高达11.1%，在镜子密集环境中的三维重建任务中表现尤为有效。

Conclusion: MVMD通过利用多视角信息显著提升了镜子检测性能，为三维重建中的镜子处理问题提供了有效解决方案，特别适用于计算机视觉中镜子密集场景的三维重建精度提升。

Abstract: In 3D reconstruction, mirrors introduce significant challenges by creating distorted and fragmented spaces, resulting in inaccurate and unreliable 3D models. As 3D reconstruction typically relies on multi-view images to capture different perspectives of a scene, detecting and labeling mirrors in multi-view images before reconstruction can effectively address this issue. However, existing methods focus solely on single-image detection, overlooking the rich information provided by multi-view setups. To overcome this limitation, we propose MVMD, a novel Multi-View Mirror Detection method, along with the first database specifically designed for mirror detection in multi-view scenes.
  The design of MVMD is grounded in the inherent associations between objects seen from different views and those reflected inside and outside of mirrors. These relationships are learned through cross- and self-attention mechanisms. MVMD consists of three key blocks: the Inter-Views Block tracks the shifts of objects within mirrors caused by changes in viewpoint; the Intra-View Block detects object reflections inside mirrors; and the Refinement Block sharpens mirror boundaries and enhances detected details.
  Experimental results show that our method improves accuracy by up to 2.6% and IoU by up to 11.1%, compared to single-image mirror detection techniques. This substantial improvement makes MVMD particularly effective for computer vision tasks, especially in enhancing the accuracy of 3D reconstruction in mirror-dense environments.

</details>


### [14] [XEns-CKD: An Explainable Ensemble-Based Approach for Chronic Kidney Disease Stage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07561)
*Rehan Ahmad,Gousia Habib,Muhammad Shaban,Ishfaq Ahmad Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XEns-CKD，一种基于集成视觉Transformer（ViT）的慢性肾病（CKD）分期分类方案，利用超声图像区分正常肾脏及CKD五个阶段。三个ViT以不同训练参数训练，集成模型分类准确率达86.36%，较现有方法提升4%，并通过LIME、LRP、Attention-Min/Max等可解释人工智能技术增强模型透明度，结合注意力图识别和解释CKD进展中受累的肾脏区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病是"无声疾病"，其进展不显著影响日常生活，但早期检测CKD分期有助于患者了解肾功能状况并遵从医嘱延缓疾病进展。因此需要一种基于超声图像的CKD分期分类方法，并强调模型的可解释性以提升临床信任。

Method: 提出XEns-CKD集成方案：(1) 在私有超声图像数据集上训练三个ViT，采用不同训练参数；(2) 集成三个ViT模型完成CKD五阶段及正常肾脏状态的分类；(3) 采用宏观敏感度、特异度、精确率、F1分数、Youden指数、MCC和宏观平衡准确率评估性能；(4) 使用LIME、LRP、Attention-Min和Attention-Max等可解释AI技术解释模型决策，并将Attention-Min与Attention-Max结果结合的注意力图用于识别CKD进展中受累的肾脏区域。

Result: 集成模型整体分类准确率达86.36%。结合Attention-Min和Attention-Max的注意力图能有效识别和解释CKD从一阶段进展至另一阶段时受累的肾脏区域，并突出显示CKD进展在这些区域的影响。与现有方法相比，提出的方法在区分CKD五个阶段和正常肾脏状态方面准确率提升4%。

Conclusion: XEns-CKD通过集成ViT模型实现了对CKD分期的高准确率分类，并结合可解释AI技术增强了模型透明度和临床可信度。注意力图有效揭示了CKD进展过程中肾区的变化，为临床早期干预和病情监测提供了辅助决策工具。

Abstract: Chronic kidney disease (CKD) is a silent disease. Its progression may not significantly hamper a person's daily routine. Human kidney function can be classified as normal or as one of the five stages of CKD. Early detection of the CKD stage can help patients understand the functional status of their kidneys and follow medical advice to slow CKD progression. In this paper, we propose XEns-CKD, a novel ensemble vision transformer-based scheme for CKD stage classification using ultrasound images. Three ViTs were trained on a private ultrasound image dataset using different training parameters. The performance of each ViT was evaluated using macro sensitivity, macro specificity, macro precision, macro F1-score, macro Youden index, the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), and macro balanced accuracy. The ensemble model achieved an overall classification accuracy of 86.36%. This work also emphasizes identifying and interpreting kidney regions affected by CKD progression. Explainable artificial intelligence techniques, including LIME, LRP, Attention-Min, and Attention-Max, were used to improve model transparency and clinical trust. An attention map combining the Attention-Min and Attention-Max results effectively identified and interpreted kidney regions affected during CKD progression from one stage to another. The attention map also highlighted the effects of CKD progression in these regions. Compared with existing methods, the proposed method classified the five CKD stages and normal kidney status with a 4% improvement in accuracy.

</details>
