*Nafis Fuad,Xiaodong Qian,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出三个基于视觉-语言模型微调的洪水深度估计模型（FloodLlama-Dense、FloodLlama-MI5、FloodLlama-MI6），利用约61万张Unreal Engine 5合成的街道级图像实现厘米级洪水深度预测。其中FloodLlama-Dense实现MAE 0.40cm，而基于机制可解释性指导的稀疏变体仅微调5-6个交叉注意力层，减少86-88%可训练参数且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市洪水对交通基础设施构成日益严重的威胁，但现有系统中没有能够提供实时、街道级别、厘米级分辨率的洪水深度估算的操作系统。这一空白驱动了本文的研究。

Method: 本文提出了三个为连续洪水深度估计微调的视觉-语言模型：FloodLlama-Dense（全量微调的QLoRA基线），以及FloodLlama-MI5和FloodLlama-MI6（基于机制可解释性分析，仅微调最相关的5个或6个交叉注意力层的稀疏变体）。训练使用约61万张图像的Unreal Engine 5合成语料子集，覆盖7种车辆类型、4种天气条件和0-40cm洪水深度。机制可解释性分析结合了线性探针、logit lens、中心核对齐（CKA）和交叉注意力熵四种技术。

Result: FloodLlama-Dense实现MAE 0.40cm、RMSE 1.97cm、Acc@5cm 97.59%。机制可解释性分析揭示了L13-L22层重组视觉表征、L23层才开始线性可解码的两阶段适应模式。FloodLlama-MI5和MI6仅微调5-6个交叉注意力层，减少86-88%可训练参数（从54.4M降至6.55-7.86M）。在真实世界基准上，FloodLlama-MI6达到98.62%准确率，远超STURM-FloodDepth基线的86.61%。

Conclusion: 本文证明机制可解释性分析能够有效指导视觉-语言模型的稀疏微调，在显著减少计算开销的同时保持甚至提升洪水深度估计性能，为街道级实时洪水监测提供了实用可行的方案。

Abstract: Urban flooding poses an escalating threat to transportation infrastructure, yet no operational system provides real-time, street-level flood-depth estimates at centimeter resolution. This paper presents three vision-language models fine-tuned for continuous flood-depth estimation from street-level imagery: FloodLlama-Dense, a fully fine-tuned QLoRA baseline, and FloodLlama-MI5 and FloodLlama-MI6, interpretability-guided sparse variants that fine-tune only the top five and six causally relevant cross-attention layers identified through mechanistic interpretability analysis, respectively. Training uses an approximately 610,000-image subset of a 2.81-million-image synthetic corpus generated in Unreal Engine 5. The dataset combines single-vehicle subsets with 5 cm depth increments and mixed-vehicle subsets with 1 cm depth increments, spanning seven vehicle types, four weather conditions, and flood depths from 0 to 40 cm. FloodLlama-Dense achieves an MAE of 0.40 cm, an RMSE of 1.97 cm, and an Acc@5cm of 97.59%. Mechanistic interpretability analysis combining linear probing, logit lens, centered kernel alignment (CKA), and cross-attention entropy reveals a two-stage adaptation pattern: layers L13-L22 restructure visual representations, while depth first becomes linearly decodable at layer L23. FloodLlama-MI5 and FloodLlama-MI6 leverage this insight by fine-tuning only five or six of the eight cross-attention layers, achieving an 86-88% reduction in trainable parameters (6.55-7.86 million versus 54.4 million) with minimal accuracy loss. On a real-world benchmark, FloodLlama-MI6 achieves 98.62% accuracy, compared with 86.61% for the published STURM-FloodDepth baseline.

</details>


### [16] [What to Edit Next: Visually Aligned Image-Editing Follow-Up Suggestions in Conversational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07565)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jinpeng Yu,Xin Song,Bingnan Li,Chuyue Li,Changhui Du,Xiaolin Fang,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段框架，为图像创作对话提供多模态后续编辑建议。基于10万条真实多轮对话数据，结合SFT微调、多目标强化学习和视觉验证器，将视觉不一致率从3.7%降至0.9%，推荐CTR提升32.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话助手主要针对纯文本交互，图像创作对话场景下的后续编辑建议未被充分探索。在该场景中，好的后续编辑建议需要反映用户偏好、提供多样化方向，并且能在当前图像上可执行，这需要多模态推荐能力。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 基于真实在线数据构建人工审核的后续编辑意图表，创建SFT目标并微调多模态策略；(2) 利用用户点击反馈通过多目标强化学习优化策略，使规则引导的SFT建议对齐真实用户选择；(3) 引入视觉验证器作为额外训练监督，减少建议编辑与当前图像之间的视觉不一致。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估上均显著超越基线。百万级用户在线A/B测试中，视觉不一致率从3.7%降至0.9%，推荐CTR提升32.70%，图像采纳率提升16.32%，平均每人对话轮次提升39.90%（均p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 该三阶段框架有效解决了图像创作对话中的后续编辑推荐问题，兼顾用户偏好对齐、方向多样性和视觉可执行性，并在大规模真实用户场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Conversational assistants increasingly recommend follow-up edits to help users continue a task. Existing systems primarily target text-only interactions, leaving image-creation conversations underexplored. In image-creation tasks, useful follow-up edit suggestions must reflect user preferences, offer diverse directions, and remain executable on the current image. We collected 100,000 real multi-turn image-creation conversation samples from Qwen App and found that 80.1% are image-dependent, underscoring the need for multimodal recommendation. We address this setting with a three-stage framework. In Stage 1, we use real online data to build a human-reviewed table of appropriate follow-up editing intents, then create SFT targets and fine-tune a multimodal policy. In Stage 2, to align rule-guided SFT suggestions with actual user choices, we use user click feedback to optimize the policy through multi-objective reinforcement learning. In Stage 3, to reduce visual inconsistencies between suggested edits and the current image, we introduce a visual verifier as additional training supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms baselines on both automatic and human evaluations. In a live user-randomized A/B test with millions of users, our final framework reduces visual inconsistency from 3.7% to 0.9%. Furthermore, it significantly improves recommendation CTR by 32.70%, image take-away rate by 16.32%, and average conversation turns per user by 39.90% (all p<0.05).

</details>


### [17] [Temporal Generalization in fNIRS-Based Autism Classification: A Cross-Time-Window Transfer Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07567)
*Marios Petrov,Sahana Vinayak,Targol Bakhtiarvand,Moses Smith Guddah,Adham Atyabi,Frederick Shic,Kevin A. Pelphrey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将fNIRS自闭症分类中的时间窗口差异形式化为跨时间窗口迁移问题，通过引入不同窗口长度（2.5-10s）和偏移的实验协议，系统评估了多种视觉架构、零样本基线和八种适应策略，发现零样本性能接近随机水平（54-69%），而约5%的受试者特定微调即可恢复90-96%的准确率，域对抗和自监督策略在无目标受试者数据下可达78-90%，且短至2.5秒的时间窗口仍包含可恢复的判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fNIRS自闭症分类方法假设时间对齐评估，但实际应用中不同受试者因血流动力学延迟和神经血管耦合差异导致最优观测窗口各不相同，存在时间分布偏移，严重降低模型性能。作者希望解决这一实际部署中的关键问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为跨时间窗口迁移问题，提出基于fNIRS地形图表示的变化窗口长度（2.5-10s）与偏移的实验协议。在124名受试者上采用留一受试者交叉验证，对三种视觉架构在不同零样本基线和八种适应策略下进行系统基准测试。

Result: (1)零样本跨窗口准确率接近随机水平（54-69%）；(2)约5%受试者特定微调可达90-96%，而受试者特定上界达97-100%，表明受试者间变异性是主要障碍；(3)域对抗和自监督策略在无目标数据下可达78-90%；(4)短至2.5秒的窗口仍可提取判别信息。

Conclusion: 研究为fNIRS自闭症分类器在真实时间变化条件下的部署提供了实用路线图，明确了受试者间变异性为性能瓶颈，并验证了无需目标数据即可获得可观性能的适应策略以及超短期窗口的信息价值。

Abstract: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) is a promising modality for autism spectrum disorder (ASD) classification, yet existing approaches assume temporally aligned evaluation. In practice, the optimal observation window varies across subjects due to differences in hemodynamic delay and neurovascular coupling, creating a temporal distribution shift that degrades performance. We formalize this as a \textit{cross-time-window transfer problem}, introducing a protocol that varies window length (2.5--10\,s) and offset within biological motion trials. Using topographic map representations of fNIRS recordings, we benchmark three vision architectures under two zero-shot baselines and eight adaptation strategies under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation ($N{=}124$). Key findings: (1) zero-shot cross-window accuracy is near chance (54--69\%); (2) ${\approx}5\%$ subject-specific fine-tuning recovers 90--96\%, while a subject-specific upper bound reaches 97--100\%, identifying inter-subject variability as the dominant barrier; (3) domain-adversarial and self-supervised strategies achieve 78--90\% without target-subject data; and (4) discriminative information is recoverable from windows as short as 2.5\,s. These findings provide a practical roadmap for deploying fNIRS-based ASD classifiers under realistic temporal variability.

</details>


### [18] [Latent-Frequency Validity: Fast Spectral Editing with Screened Video-VAE Transfer Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07569)
*Bowen Xue,Jiafeng Xiong,Xin Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"潜频有效性"(LFV)方法，通过学习VAE特定的紧凑谱响应，在视频VAE潜空间中进行直接谱编辑，避免了解码-滤波-重编码的额外开销。LFV采用验证选择的路径（从对角逐频校准器C1到全通道混合CM），将跨通道容量作为可控的逐编辑资源，实验表明其生成的算子绝大部分通过保留验证，且延迟比像素滤波-重编码快约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在视频VAE潜空间中进行谱编辑（控制噪声、闪烁、平滑度及频率内容）相比传统解码-滤波-重编码流程更高效。然而视频VAE会跨潜通道重新分布像素空间的频带，潜空间的编辑可能破坏VAE往返动态，因此需要一种能够感知VAE特定谱响应的编辑方法。

Method: 提出latent-frequency validity (LFV)框架：1) 学习一个紧凑的VAE特定谱响应模型；2) 仅在能提升解码目标保真度且不恶化往返漂移时部署该响应；3) 通过验证选择路径，从对角逐频校准器(C1)逐步升级到全通道混合(CM)，将跨通道容量作为可控制的逐编辑资源。

Result: 在544个VAE-编辑单元上，LFV产出423个廉价算子：277个由C1处理，146个需要通道混合。主要120单元径向扫描中99/100个发射算子通过源视频分组保留验证；五个额外滤波器族中全部323个发射算子通过验证。冻结的OpenVid拟合算子（含验证选择路径系数）在全部20个CogVideoX和HunyuanVideo生成域单元上无需适配即通过。延迟与直接潜滤波相当，比像素滤波-重编码快约3倍。

Conclusion: LFV揭示了不同VAE的不同频谱响应机制，包括强通道耦合的CogVideoX响应和Open-Sora尖锐的高频稳定性边界，证明了验证选择驱动的混合路由策略能有效平衡编辑保真度与计算开销。

Abstract: Direct spectral editing in video-VAE latents can control noise, flicker, smoothness, and frequency content without a decode--filter--reencode pass. However, video VAEs may redistribute pixel-space frequency bands across latent channels, and latent edits can disrupt VAE round-trip dynamics. We introduce \emph{latent-frequency validity} (LFV), which learns a compact VAE-specific spectral response and deploys it only when it improves decoded-target fidelity without worsening round-trip drift. LFV follows a validation-selected path from a diagonal per-frequency calibrator (C1) to full channel mixing (CM), making cross-channel capacity a controllable per-edit resource. Across 544 VAE--edit cells spanning six spectral families, LFV emits 423 cheap operators: 277 are handled by C1, while 146 (34.5\% of emitted operators) require channel mixing. On the primary 120-cell radial sweep, 99/100 emitted operators pass source-video-grouped held-out evaluation. Across five additional filter families, all 323 emitted operators pass held-out evaluation. Fully frozen OpenVid-fitted operators, including the validation-selected path coefficient, pass all 20 tested CogVideoX and HunyuanVideo generated-domain cells without adaptation. The selected response matches direct latent-filter latency and is about $3\times$ faster than pixel filter--reencode. The resulting maps reveal distinct VAE regimes, including strongly channel-coupled CogVideoX responses and a sharp Open-Sora high-band stability frontier.

</details>


### [19] [COMEX: A Composition-Grounded Benchmark and Learning Framework for Explainable Aesthetic Image Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07570)
*Rui Yang,Wei Zhou,Dingyong Gou,Xiaohui Cui,Cong Li,Yinyin Gong,Yipo Huang,Jiliang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新定义了可解释美学图像裁剪问题为结构化的"裁剪-构图-解释"任务，提出了新的基准COMEX和两阶段SFT+GRPO训练框架，在15个大型视觉语言模型上进行了系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"裁剪并解释"的方法大多将解释视为事后文本生成，忽视了构图这一连接裁剪决策与可解释推理的关键美学因素。

Method: 1) 将可解释美学裁剪重构为裁剪-构图-解释的结构化问题；2) 通过图像扩展和IO反转管道构建COMEX基准，包含33,161个四元组（扩展图像、裁剪框、构图类别、构图驱动的解释）；3) 提出两阶段SFT+GRPO框架，先用监督微调建立结构化输出协议和基础裁剪能力，再用GRPO提升裁剪质量、构图预测和解释忠实度。

Result: 在COMEX基准上评测了15个大型视觉语言模型和现有裁剪方法，构建了全面的测试平台；实验在COMEX及先前基准上验证了框架的有效性和可迁移性，各评估指标上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 构图驱动的结构化可解释裁剪框架有效联合学习了裁剪定位、构图理解和解释生成，具有良好的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Explainable aesthetic image cropping requires not only localizing a visually pleasing crop but also explaining why it is preferred. Existing crop-and-explain methods largely treat explanation as post-hoc text generation and overlook composition, a key aesthetic factor that links crop decisions with interpretable reasoning. In this paper, we reformulate explainable aesthetic image cropping as a structured crop-composition-explanation problem. To support this setting, we introduce COMEX, a new benchmark built through image expansion and an IO-reversal pipeline. COMEX contains 33,161 quadruples, each consisting of an expanded image, a crop box, a composition category, and a composition-grounded explanation, enabling joint learning of crop localization, composition understanding, and explanation generation. We further propose a two-stage SFT+GRPO framework, where supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured output protocol and basic cropping ability, and GRPO further improves crop quality, composition prediction, and explanation faithfulness. We benchmark 15 large vision-language models and existing cropping methods on COMEX, establishing a comprehensive testbed for composition-grounded explainable aesthetic cropping. Experiments on both COMEX and prior benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and transferability of our framework, with strong performance across evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [20] [A Review of Vision-Based Vehicle Detection for UAV-Based Traffic Monitoring: Experimental Insights and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07571)
*Jianlin Ye,Christos Kyrkou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于无人机（UAV）的智能交通监控系统，聚焦于深度学习模型在交通分析中的应用，识别了三大挑战（与交通控制系统兼容、实时处理、不同环境条件下的鲁棒检测），并指出现有方案缺乏综合利用UAV数据应对事故和交通管理的完整框架，未来研究应聚焦于优化检测模型、边缘处理和自适应控制集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能交通系统中，无人机监控相比固定地面基础设施具有广覆盖和实时数据采集的优势，且能响应动态交通变化。然而，无人机在实际部署中面临不同海拔下车辆检测、运动引起的图像补偿、以及高分辨率图像高效处理等挑战。尽管深度学习显著提升了检测精度，但在实际应用中对精度、延迟及与现有交通系统的协调的批判性评估仍然不足，因此需要系统性的综述来梳理该领域进展和缺口。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）方法，系统性回顾了基于深度神经网络模型的无人机交通监控的最新进展，重点梳理了在多种城市环境下的交通分析应用，并从文献中归纳出三大核心挑战：与现有交通控制系统的兼容性、实现实时处理以优化交通流的可行性，以及不同环境条件下的鲁棒目标检测能力。同时分析了现有方案在利用无人机数据应对交通事件和交通管理方面的局限性。

Result: 综述识别了该领域文献中的三大主要挑战：（1）与交通控制系统的兼容性；（2）实时处理能力以满足交通流优化需求；（3）在不同环境条件下维持鲁棒检测性能。此外，研究发现现有解决方案缺乏完整的框架来利用无人机捕获的数据进行事件响应和交通管理，指出了从检测模型、边缘计算到自适应控制集成方面的技术缺口。

Conclusion: 未来研究应重点关注三个方面：最优检测模型的设计与选择、边缘处理技术的应用以降低延迟，以及自适应控制集成以提升城市交通管理的响应能力。通过这些方向的推进，才能充分发挥无人机监控在智能交通系统中的潜力。

Abstract: In Intelligent Transportation System (ITS), unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based surveillance offers an innovative solution to traffic surveillance with wide coverage and real-time data collection capabilities. In comparison to fixed ground-based infrastructure, UAVs are able to respond to dynamic traffic but present challenges such as vehicle detection at varying altitudes, compensation for motion-induced image variations and efficient processing of high-resolution images. Deep learning has been largely beneficial on improving the detection accuracy; however, for practical deployment, a critical assessment of the accuracy, latency, and harmonization with current transportation systems needs to be carefully considered. This survey reviews recent advancements in the UAV-based traffic monitoring, with a primary focus being deep neural network models for traffic analytics in various urban settings. Three main challenges identified in the literature are ensuring compatibility with traffic control systems, achieving real-time processing to optimize traffic flow, and maintaining robust detection in different environmental conditions. Existing solutions often lack comprehensive frameworks for utilizing UAV captured data to respond to incidents and manage traffic effectively. Future research should focus on optimal detection models, edge processing, and adaptive control integration to improve the responsiveness of urban traffic management.

</details>


### [21] [BRACE: Taming Sharp Irregularities via Barycentric Rational Forecasting for Fast Diffusion Transformers Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07572)
*Jinlong Yang,Jinke Wu,Lizilin,Yao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BRACE是一种基于重心有理函数与Chebyshev增强的扩散Transformer（DiT）时间特征缓存方法，旨在解决现有基于导数多项式外推在高速加速下导致的生成质量严重退化问题，实现了高质量与高效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在图像和视频生成中表现优异，但其计算开销巨大。现有的时间特征缓存方法（cache-then-forecast）采用基于导数的多项式外推，在高加速场景下因长步长预测不稳定而导致严重的生成质量退化。

Method: BRACE将范式从导数驱动的多项式外推转变为特征驱动的有理数预测。它维护一个局部滑动窗口缓存稀疏历史特征，利用自适应Chebyshev权重构造重心有理函数，直接聚合原始特征以确保数值稳定性。

Result: 大量实验表明，BRACE在多种DiT架构上实现了最先进的质量-效率权衡，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过将预测策略从导数驱动的外推转为特征驱动的有理数预测，BRACE有效应对了DiT特征轨迹全局平滑但局部非光滑的特性，显著提升了高加速场景下的生成稳定性和质量。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have demonstrated exceptional performance in high-fidelity image and video generation. To alleviate their massive computational overhead, temporal feature caching has been proposed to bypass redundant computations. However, existing cache-then-forecast methods driven by derivative-based polynomials often cause severe quality degradation under high acceleration due to unstable long-step predictions. To address this bottleneck, we propose Barycentric Rational Forecasting with Chebyshev Enhancement (BRACE). Motivated by the observation that DiT feature trajectories are globally smooth yet frequently exhibit sharp irregularities and local non-smoothness, BRACE shifts the paradigm from derivative-driven polynomial extrapolation to feature-driven rational forecasting. Specifically, it maintains a local sliding window to cache sparse historical features and leverages adapted Chebyshev weights to formulate a barycentric rational function, directly aggregating these raw features to ensure numerical stability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BRACE achieves state-of-the-art quality-efficiency trade-offs across various DiT architectures with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [22] [Multimodal Skin Lesion Classification with Swin Transformer and Clinical Metadata Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07574)
*Nethmi Pathirana,Isuru Munasinghe,Dileeka Alwis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态皮肤病变分类框架，融合Swin Transformer图像特征与结构化临床元数据，在公开数据集上达到92.55%的测试准确率和91.33%的宏F1分数，并通过温度缩放校准、不确定性估计和可解释性分析提升模型的可靠性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病变分类对皮肤癌早期诊断至关重要，但皮肤镜图像中的类别不平衡、类间相似性和类内变异性使自动化分析面临挑战，需要更有效的分类方法。

Method: 提出多模态分类框架，将基于Swin Transformer的图像特征与结构化临床元数据相结合，实现视觉-上下文信息的集成学习；采用温度缩放作为后处理校准方法降低期望校准误差，并引入不确定性估计评估预测置信度，辅以定性可解释性分析。

Result: 在公开数据集上测试准确率达92.55%，宏F1分数为91.33%，在少数类上表现优异；温度缩放降低了期望校准误差，提升预测可靠性；可解释性分析显示模型在推理时聚焦于病变区域。

Conclusion: 多模态融合结合校准与可解释性分析，为自动化皮肤病变分类提供了一种有效且可信的方法。

Abstract: Skin lesion classification plays an important role in supporting the early diagnosis of skin cancer. However, automated analysis remains challenging due to class imbalance, inter-class similarity, and intra-class variability in dermoscopic images. This paper proposes a multimodal classification framework that combines Swin Transformer-based image features with structured clinical metadata to improve diagnostic performance through integrated visual-context learning. Experiments on a publicly available dataset show that the proposed model achieves a test accuracy of 92.55% and a macro F1-score of 91.33%, with strong performance across minority classes. Temperature scaling is applied as a post-hoc calibration method, resulting in a reduction in expected calibration error and improving prediction reliability, while uncertainty estimation is incorporated to further assess the confidence of model predictions. Qualitative explainability analysis further shows that the model focuses on lesion regions during inference. Therefore, the results demonstrate that multimodal fusion, combined with calibration and interpretability analysis, provides an effective and trustworthy approach for automated skin lesion classification.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Isotropic Assumptions: Continuity-Constrained Segmentation and GPU Morphometry for Nanoscale GBM Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07575)
*Arash Fatehi,Robin Ebbestad,Linus Butt,Hans Blom,Sigrid Lundberg,Hannes Olauson,Hjalmar Brismar,David Unnersjö-Jess,Thomas Benzing,Katarzyna Bozek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的GPU加速框架，在不添加额外标注的情况下，直接在原生各向异性采集体积上训练分割模型，通过随机旋转训练块（利用横向高分辨率平面提供轴向缺失信息）和z轴连续性损失来解决共聚焦显微镜图像在各向异性3D分割中的挑战。该方法在肾小球基底膜（GBM）分割上达到与专家间一致性相当的水平，并实现了全自动的各向异性3D形态计量分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共聚焦显微镜对光学透明化及膨胀组织进行3D成像时，轴向采样严重不足导致轴向方向结构不连续，妨碍了重建和自动量化分析。传统解决方案是先将轴向维度上采样为各向同性体积再训练分割模型，但这需要在升采样空间中做密集标注，标注负担过重且不切实际。

Method: 1) 直接在原生采集体积上训练模型，无需额外标注；2) 通过随机旋转训练块利用横向高分辨率平面补充轴向缺失信息；3) 引入z轴连续性损失保持相邻切片一致性；4) 适配卷积（3D U-Net）和Transformer（SwinUNETR）两种骨干网络；5) 通过高斯共识聚合重叠块；6) 在GPU上通过射线-曲面相交计算经过点扩散函数（PSF）校正的膜厚度。

Result: 1) 分割精度达到专家间一致性水平；2) 连续性感知训练显著提升重建平滑度并抑制周期性阶梯状伪影（terracing artifact），且仅以极小的精度为代价；3) 在重建的3D表面上量化GBM厚度，成功捕捉疾病相关的增厚变化；4) 实现了全自动的各向异性3D生物结构形态计量分析，无需密集体积标注或图像恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作证明可以在不进行各向同性上采样和密集标注的前提下，直接在各向异性采集数据上实现高质量3D分割与形态计量，为薄而高度卷曲的生物膜结构（如肾小球基底膜）提供了一种完全自动化的疾病相关形态变化量化工具。

Abstract: Confocal microscopy of optically cleared and swelled tissue resolves complex biological structures in 3D, but such acquisitions are highly anisotropic: along the under-sampled axial direction the structure can appear discontinuous, hampering reconstruction and automated quantitative analysis. The usual remedy upsamples the axial dimension to an isotropic volume before training a segmentation model, which requires dense annotations in the upsampled space, a prohibitive labeling burden.
  We present an end-to-end, GPU-accelerated framework that overcomes this without additional annotations. The model is trained on the native acquisition volume; random rotation of training patches leverages the well-resolved lateral plane to supply the missing axial information, and a z-axis continuity loss keeps neighboring slices consistent. We adapt both a convolutional (3D U-Net) and a transformer (SwinUNETR) backbone, aggregate overlapping patches by Gaussian consensus, and compute point-spread-function-corrected membrane thickness by ray-surface intersection on the GPU.
  We apply the method to the glomerular basement membrane (GBM), a thin, highly convoluted part of the kidney's filtration barrier that grows more irregular in disease. Segmentation accuracy matches inter-expert agreement. Continuity-aware training improves reconstruction smoothness and suppresses a periodic terracing artifact at minimal accuracy cost. We quantify GBM thickness across the reconstructed 3D surface and capture disease-related thickening, enabling fully automated anisotropic 3D morphometry of biological structures without dense volumetric labels or image restoration.

</details>


### [24] [Open-World Hierarchical Perception: Taxonomic Abstraction over Class-Agnostic Proposals for the Safe Handling of Out-of-Vocabulary Road Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07577)
*Felix Schaller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶的开放世界感知层：在类无关的区域提案之上叠加层次化分类与运行时抽象规则，使得封闭集检测器无法框出的物体也能被归类或标记为未知障碍物。在留出7个COCO类别的235个真实标注目标上进行评估，层次化方法实现了0%的置信错误特定标签（平面封闭模型为100%），但正确超类恢复率仅26%，其余94%被保守处理。作者明确这是安全性成果而非特异性成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的封闭集检测器只能给对象赋予固定标签集合中的标签，当遇到集合外的物体（如马车、路面杂物、牲畜）时只能给出错误的确定性标签或将其丢弃。此前系列工作提出了层次化分类体系与运行时抽象规则，但仅在封闭检测器已产生的候选框上评估，未能真正处理检测器未框出的物体。本文希望将层次化抽象置于类无关区域提案之上，真正实现开放世界感知，并弥补此前无法进行的地面真值留出类基准测试。

Method: 本文将层次化分类抽象（taxonomic abstraction）叠加在类无关的区域提案（class-agnostic region proposals）之上，使得封闭检测器遗漏的物体仍能被分类或标记。具体包括三方面：（1）对三种开放世界信号进行可行性研究——类无关分割、基于外观的分布外评分、单目深度，分析为何单一2D线索不足以及它们如何组合；（2）提出运行时抽象规则，在层次化分类体系下对超出词汇表外的目标进行安全处理；（3）执行此前论文无法完成的地面真值留出类基准评估：留出COCO 7个类别，对235个真实标注目标裁剪进行分类测试。

Result: 留出7个COCO类别并对235个地面真值目标进行分类时：（1）平面封闭检测头100%输出置信但错误的特定标签，其中37%落入错误的超类（如动物被命名为车辆）；（2）层次化感知层输出0个置信错误特定标签，94%的对象被安全处理（正确的超类或明确的UNKNOWN OBSTACLE标记）；（3）但正确的超类仅恢复26%，其余69%被保守标记为未知。开放世界信号的组合可行性研究揭示了单一2D线索的不足及其互补性。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个开放世界感知层，其对词汇表外的物体绝不产生置信的分类错误，并诚实地报告了其代价（正确超类恢复率低至26%，大量对象被保守标记为未知）。作者明确指出这是一个安全结果而非特异性结果——系统通过牺牲分类特异性来换取分类安全性，为自动驾驶开放世界感知提供了可靠且可审计的设计。

Abstract: A closed-set detector for autonomous driving must assign every object one of a fixed set of labels. On an object outside that set (a horse-drawn carriage, road debris, livestock on a rural road) it can only force a confident but wrong specific label or drop the object. Prior work in this series replaced the flat label set with a hierarchical taxonomy and a runtime abstraction rule, but evaluated it only on the boxes a closed detector already produces. This paper takes the layer open-world: we place taxonomic abstraction on top of class-agnostic region proposals so objects the closed detector never boxes can still be classified or flagged; we report a feasibility study of three open-world signals (class-agnostic segmentation, appearance-based out-of-distribution scoring, monocular depth) that shows why no single 2D cue suffices and how they compose; and we run the evaluation the earlier papers could not, a ground-truth leave-classes-out benchmark on real annotated objects. Holding out seven COCO classes and classifying their 235 ground-truth crops, a flat closed head emits a confident wrong specific label 100% of the time (37% of them in the wrong super-category, e.g. an animal named as a vehicle), whereas the hierarchical layer emits zero confident wrong specific labels and safely handles 94% of the objects (a correct super-category, or an explicit UNKNOWN OBSTACLE). We are explicit that this is a safety result, not a specificity one: the correct super-category is recovered only 26% of the time and the remaining 69% are conservatively flagged unknown. The contribution is an open-world perception layer that never makes a confident categorical mistake on an out-of-vocabulary object, together with an honest account of its cost.

</details>


### [25] [Geometry Beats Estimated Depth: RGB-Only Multi-Camera 3D Tracking under Sim2Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07579)
*Abdullah Naeem,Anav Katwal,Ayon Dey,Noman Khan,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1中多相机3D感知的Sim2Real问题（推理时仅RGB）。通过对比"几何优先"流水线与"伪LiDAR"流水线，发现跨视角几何一致性（而非单目深度精度）决定性能：几何优先达到13.0 3D HOTA，而伪LiDAR仅0.12。作者认为瓶颈互补——检测质量限制几何路线，定位一致性限制伪LiDAR路线，并发布了完整可复现的RGB-only流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Sim2Real设置下评估多相机3D室内感知，深度仅在训练/验证时可用而推理时仅RGB，旨在验证"跨视角几何一致性而非单目深度精度主导Sim2Real性能"这一假设，并为该任务提供可控的实验基准。

Method: 设计两条RGB-only路线：(1) 几何优先流水线：YOLO11x检测+单应性提升到世界坐标系+类别级3D尺寸先验+多相机融合+世界坐标跟踪+离线轨迹拼接；(2) 伪LiDAR：单目深度（D4RT、Metric3D v2）反投影融合点云并通过V-DETR 3D检测器处理。同时系统消融了SAHI检测、外观Re-ID、学习式提升、RT-DETR集成、测试时增强和域随机化等干预手段。

Result: 几何优先路线达13.0 3D HOTA (51.6 LocA)，伪LiDAR路线崩塌至0.12 (9.2 LocA)。深度崩坏原因归结为单目深度的跨视角不一致性——尺度校正是必要但不充分条件，域适应微调在预算内无法修复。在几何流水线中，仅离线轨迹拼接有效，其余所有干预均未超越基线检测器。

Conclusion: 跨视角几何一致性是Sim2Real多相机3D感知中的决定性因素；单目深度准确度不能代表跨视角一致性。检测质量（DetA）限制几何路线，定位一致性（LocA）限制伪LiDAR路线，两条路线的瓶颈互补。作者发布了完整可复现的RGB-only流水线及消融实验。

Abstract: The AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1 evaluates multi-camera 3D perception in large indoor warehouses under a synthetic-to-real (Sim2Real) setting; depth is available only for training and validation, so inference is RGB-only. We use two RGB-only routes as a controlled test of one hypothesis: that cross-view geometric consistency, not monocular depth accuracy, governs performance under Sim2Real. The first is a geometry-first pipeline: YOLO11x detection, homography lifting to the world frame, class-level 3D size priors, multi-camera fusion, world-coordinate tracking, and offline tracklet stitching. The second is estimated-depth pseudo-LiDAR: monocular depth (D4RT, Metric3D~v2) back-projected into a fused point cloud and passed to a 3D detector (V-DETR), mirroring prior point-cloud winners that used depth. The gap is decisive: geometry-first reaches 13.0 3D HOTA (51.6 LocA), whereas pseudo-LiDAR collapses to 0.12 (9.2 LocA). We trace the collapse to cross-view inconsistency of monocular depth---scale correction is necessary but not sufficient---which domain-adaptation fine-tuning does not repair within budget. Within the geometry pipeline, offline stitching is the only intervention that helps; SAHI detection, appearance Re-ID, learned lifting, RT-DETR ensembling, test-time augmentation, and domain randomization all fail to beat the baseline detector. The bottlenecks are complementary: detection quality bounds the geometry route (DetA), localization consistency bounds pseudo-LiDAR (LocA). We release a complete, reproducible RGB-only pipeline and ablation.

</details>


### [26] [Real-time physics inversion for retrieval of sub-pixel wildfire temperatures from VSWIR imaging spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07580)
*William R. Keely,Philip G. Brodrick,Katherine Mistick,Adam Chlus,Robert O. Green,Philip E. Dennison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全物理正向模型的野火温度反演框架，应用于NASA AVIRIS-3 VSWIR成像光谱数据。该框架利用非线性最小二乘方法在GPU上快速收敛，在168架次2025年FireSense任务中实现了有效火灾温度估计，SWIR波段残差拟合≤10%，并验证了后验参数对EMIT等星载成像光谱仪的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火温度是理解燃烧动态、火灾强度和灾害影响的关键参数，但现有反演方法往往在计算效率和精度之间难以兼顾。随着AVIRIS-3等多光谱成像数据的获取能力提升，需要一种既能利用全光谱信息、又能满足飞行节奏内实时处理需求的全物理反演框架。

Method: 采用全物理正向模型，同时解析太阳辐射和火灾发射辐射（源自温度分布），并在残差拟合中利用全光谱范围。使用最先进的非线性最小二乘方法，在机载GPU上实现快速收敛，从而在飞行节奏内估计有效火灾温度。方法包括模拟光谱验证（注入热信号）和面向空间分辨率粗化的泛化验证。

Result: 模拟光谱验证中，反演与真实值吻合良好，RMSE为41.8 K；在2025年FireSense AVIRIS-3战役共168架次过航中，SWIR波段残差辐射拟合≤10%；在空间分辨率粗化至EMIT级别后，后验分布在分位数上对亚像元温度范围的绝对误差为30 K，不同空间分辨率间的平均绝对误差为27.16 K。

Conclusion: 该框架在机载VSWIR成像光谱数据上实现了快速、高精度的野火温度反演，且其反演参数可泛化至星载成像光谱仪（如EMIT），为跨平台的野火温度监测提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In this work, we present a wildfire temperature retrieval framework for VSWIR imaging spectroscopy data, employed on data from NASA's Airborne Visible Infrared Imaging Spectrometer (AVIRIS-3). The retrieval framework utilizes a full-physics approach in which a forward model is employed to resolve both solar and emitted radiance derived from a temperature distribution and utilizes the full spectral range in the residual fit. To optimize the forward model retrieval, we use state-of-the-art nonlinear least squares methods implemented for fast convergence on the on-board GPU, allowing for estimation of effective fire temperature within flight cadence. We verify the forward model assumptions on simulated spectra with an injected thermal signature and find good agreement with an RMSE of $41.8$ Kelvin (K). We apply the retrieval over the full 2025 FireSense AVIRIS-3 campaign, totaling 168 overflights with probable active fire spectra, and demonstrate a residual radiance fit of $\leq 10\%$ across bands in the short-wave infrared (SWIR). Lastly, we verify the applicability of the retrieved posterior fire temperature parameters to generalize to space-borne imaging spectrometers such as EMIT, by retrieving at coarsened spatial resolution. We find that the posterior distribution exhibits good coverage of the underlying sub-pixel temperature range with an absolute error of $30$ K across quantiles and a mean absolute error of $27.16$ K between spatial resolutions.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Branch Policy Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07581)
*Shuai Lyu,Yuning Gong,Ruiling Gao,Xiaoran Shang,Zhonghong Ou,Ping Zong,Yifan Zhu,Yuan Sun,Yang Qin,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多分支策略优化（MBPO）框架，通过构建推理树将轨迹级信用分配细化为段级分支相对优势，解决多模态大语言模型在感知不确定性下的信用分配退化问题，并引入时间重放缓冲区提升优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于组的强化学习方法对多模态大语言模型进行轨迹级信用分配，即为响应中的所有token赋予同一优势值。然而多模态推理涉及远高于纯文本场景的感知不确定性，模型需反复核查视觉信息来验证中间解释，不同的视觉基础可导致发散推理路径，使得这种均匀信用分配不充分，导致相对优势逐渐退化为零。

Method: 提出多分支策略优化（MBPO）框架：1）在视觉-语言决策边界构建推理树，让兄弟分支探索多样的视觉假设；2）通过分支相对优势实现段级信用分配；3）引入时间重放缓冲区复用信息段，同时控制策略陈旧性。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，MBPO优于代表性基线方法，在提升学习信号质量和优化效率两方面均有改善。

Conclusion: MBPO通过树结构分支探索和段级信用分配有效解决了多模态推理中感知不确定性导致的学习信号退化问题，并显著提升了优化效率。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning methods for multimodal large language models typically rely on trajectory-level credit assignment that applies a single advantage to all tokens in a response. However, multimodal reasoning involves substantially higher perceptual uncertainty than text-only settings, where the model must repeatedly re-examine visual information to verify intermediate interpretations, and different visual groundings can lead to divergent reasoning paths, making such uniform credit assignment particularly inadequate and causing relative advantages to progressively degenerate toward zero. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-Branch Policy Optimization (MBPO), a tree-based framework that constructs reasoning trees at vision-language decision boundaries, enabling sibling branches to explore diverse visual hypotheses and assigning segment-level credit through branch-relative advantages. We further introduce a temporal replay buffer to reuse informative segments while controlling policy staleness. Experiments on several multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that MBPO outperforms representative baselines, improving both learning signal quality and optimization efficiency. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/ShuaiLyu0110/MBPO.

</details>


### [28] [Predictive Failure Detection in Network Hardware Using Thermal Imaging and Deep Learning with Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07582)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的预测性维护策略，利用热成像与电力传感器数据检测数据中心网络设备（路由器、交换机、服务器）的早期故障征兆。通过ROI预处理和多模态CNN-LSTM融合模型，故障预测准确率达到94%，显著优于无预处理的纯CNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心网络设备意外硬件故障会导致服务中断和昂贵停机损失。传统维护策略（如定期检修）无法实现早期故障预警，亟需一种非侵入式、基于数据驱动的预测性维护方法来提前识别设备劣化迹象。

Method: 构建包含标注热成像与功率读数数据的模拟数据集，涵盖Normal、Warning和Critical三种运行状态。评估三种ImageNet预训练CNN模型（ResNet-50、InceptionV3、VGG16），并提出一种融合视觉与传感器时间序列信息的多模态CNN-LSTM模型。实验对比了有无预处理（ROI提取与归一化）两种情况下的性能表现。

Result: 无预处理时CNN模型准确率中等（ResNet-50为52%）；经ROI预处理后性能显著提升（ResNet-50达91%）；CNN-LSTM多模态融合模型取得最高准确率94%，精确率和召回率接近95%。

Conclusion: 领域特定的预处理和多模态传感器融合能显著改善早期故障预测性能。该方法为数据中心网络硬件提供了一种基于非侵入式监测的主动维护方案基础。

Abstract: Unplanned network hardware malfunctions can interrupt services and result in expensive downtime in data centers. A deep learning-based predictive maintenance strategy is presented that utilizes thermal imaging and power sensor data to detect early indicators of equipment breakdown in routers, switches, and servers. A simulated dataset was generated comprising annotated thermal pictures and power readings indicative of three operating states: Normal, Warning, and Critical. Three ImageNet-pretrained convolutional neural network (CNN) models ResNet-50, InceptionV3, and VGG16 were assessed together with a multi-modal CNN-LSTM fusion model that integrates visual and sensor time-series information. Experiments were performed with and without pre-processing procedures, including region-of-interest (ROI) extraction and normalization. In the absence of pre-processing, CNNs attained moderate accuracy (e.g., ResNet-50 at 52%), but ROI-based pre-processing significantly enhanced performance (ResNet-50 accuracy reaching 91%). The CNN-LSTM model attained the greatest accuracy of 94%, with precision and recall approaching 95%, illustrating the effectiveness of multi-modal fusion. The results validate that domain-specific pre-processing and sensor fusion substantially improve early failure prediction, providing a potential foundation for proactive maintenance of network hardware through non-intrusive monitoring.

</details>


### [29] [ComplexityWorld: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Verifiable Visual Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07584)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPLEXITYWORLD基准，包含390个任务、39个领域视觉世界和29个决策类别，用于评估视觉语言模型从视觉证据做出满足全局约束的完整决策的能力。实验发现除GPT-5.6-Sol外所有模型在直接推理下的验证器接受率（VAR）均低于40%，揭示了一个"视觉到决策"的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测多聚焦于图像内容识别，但现实世界中的许多任务要求模型不仅要识别图像内容，还要利用视觉证据做出满足全局约束的完整决策。作者希望系统性地评估VLM在这类"视觉驱动决策"任务上的能力，并探究不同提示方式、结构化信息和智能体框架对性能的影响。

Method: 构建COMPLEXITYWORLD基准：每个任务从一个隐藏的结构化规格生成，渲染为视觉场景，并由可执行验证器评分（接受任何可行解）。基准包含390个任务、39个领域视觉世界和29个决策类别。评估了三种条件下的模型表现：(1)直接推理；(2)将相同决策信息显式提供为结构化形式；(3)使用智能体脚手架辅助推理。

Result: 直接推理下，除GPT-5.6-Sol（75.6% VAR）外，所有模型的验证器接受率均低于40%。当决策信息以显式结构化形式提供时，性能显著提升，但在等价的视觉呈现之间性能波动剧烈。智能体脚手架带来的收益较小且依赖于具体模型。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一个普遍且持久的"视觉到决策"瓶颈，仅靠增加推理计算量无法消除。该瓶颈即使使用智能体脚手架也只能部分缓解，说明VLM在从视觉输入到满足全局约束的决策之间仍存在根本性的能力缺口。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made rapid progress in visual perception and increasingly support real-world tasks that depend on images. Many such tasks, however, require more than rec- ognizing what an image contains: a model must use visual evidence to make a complete decision whose parts jointly satisfy global constraints. We introduce COMPLEXITYWORLD, a benchmark of 390 tasks across 39 domain-inspired visual worlds and 29 decision categories. Each task is generated from a hidden structured specification, rendered as a visual scene, and scored by an exe- cutable verifier that accepts any feasible solution. Under direct inference, all evaluated models ex- cept GPT-5.6-Sol remain below 40% verifier ac- ceptance rate (VAR), while GPT-5.6-Sol reaches 75.6%. Performance improves substantially when the same decision information is made explicit in structured form, yet varies sharply across equiva- lent visual presentations. Agent scaffolds provide smaller, model-dependent gains. Together, these results reveal a persistent visual-to-decision bot- tleneck that additional inference alone does not remove.

</details>


### [30] [LAVE: Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning for Video Tool-use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07585)
*Zijian Wang,Junnan Zhu,Rongzhen Li,Xiao Liu,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Lijia Liu,Yining Wang,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAVE是一个无需训练的视频工具使用智能体框架，通过双通道观测接口（可见文本通道+隐式视觉潜变量通道）复用已完成工具调用中未被口头化的视觉证据，解决了Tool观测瓶颈问题，在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要模型从冗长的视频流中高效获取稀疏视觉证据。现有的视频工具使用智能体在Tool-Planner通信中依赖文本观测，但文本接口对工具计算结果是有损的摘要，导致未口头化的视觉证据被丢弃，形成"Tool观测瓶颈"。

Method: LAVE框架包含：(1) 双通道观测接口——可见通道保留原始文本轨迹，隐式通道存储带工具角色、源帧时间戳和视觉位置的预语言视觉更新；(2) 检索机制——在规划时检索与当前Planner状态相关但未被文本观测覆盖的证据；(3) 有界时间戳对齐的隐式更新与熵约束的帧时间路由，实现视觉证据的高效集成。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和CG-Bench三个基准上，LAVE在多种骨干网络上一致提升视频工具使用智能体的性能。在可比帧预算下，LAVE在Video-MME总体上比最强基线提高3.76分。

Conclusion: LAVE证明了隐式视觉证据复用对多步视频智能体规划的有效性，无需额外训练、帧重放或修改原始编排即可提升视频智能体的理解与规划能力，为视频工具使用智能体的观测接口设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Long-video understanding requires models to efficiently acquire and reuse sparse visual evidence from long and redundant video streams. Recent video tool-use agents address this challenge by iteratively invoking visual Tools at different temporal scales, but their Tool-Planner communication typically relies on textual observations. Such text-only interfaces provide lossy summaries of Tool computations, causing previously computed visual evidence not verbalized to be discarded and unavailable for subsequent planning. We identify this limitation as the Tool observation bottleneck and propose Latent Visual Evidence-Enhanced Planning (LAVE), a training-free framework for reusing latent visual evidence from completed Tool calls. LAVE introduces a dual-channel observation interface: the visible channel preserves the original textual trajectory, while the latent channel stores pre-verbal visual updates with their Tool roles, source-frame timestamps, and visual locations. During planning, LAVE retrieves evidence relevant to the current Planner state but not covered by textual observations, and integrates it through bounded timestamp-aligned latent updates with entropy-constrained frame-time routing. This enables video agents to reuse existing visual computation without additional training, frame replay, or modifications to the original orchestration. Extensive experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and CG-Bench show that LAVE consistently improves video tool-use agents across backbones. Under a comparable frame budget, LAVE improves the Video-MME overall score by 3.76 points over the strongest baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of latent visual evidence reuse for multi-step video-agent planning.

</details>


### [31] [MAGIC-SSCIL: Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration for Semi-Supervised Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07586)
*Yousef Abdi,Mohammad Asadpour,Yousef Seyfari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGIC框架，用于解决半监督类别增量学习（SSCIL）中无范例（exemplar-free）设置下的灾难性遗忘问题。MAGIC通过软加权几何校准（SWGC）和几何结构对齐（GSA）两个组件，在不存储旧数据的前提下稳定特征空间，防止漂移。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200和ImageNet-R数据集上，MAGIC在不同标签比例下均优于现有监督CIL方法和SSCIL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSCIL在无范例设置下最为困难，现有方法因特征漂移（feature drift）导致灾难性遗忘，且随着标签空间增大，伪标签（pseudo-labels）的可靠性急剧下降。这一问题在细粒度、低标签比例场景中尤为严重，传统置信度阈值方法难以有效应对，亟需一种不依赖存储旧样本的稳定增量学习框架。

Method: MAGIC包含两个核心组件：（1）软加权几何校准（SWGC）：基于图标签传播，在可学习的弹性特征空间上对冻结骨干网络计算的类均值和方差进行加权校准，并由校准后的高斯分布采样幻象特征（phantom features）以替代旧任务数据；（2）几何结构对齐（GSA）目标：通过匹配学生与教师头的相对关系结构并将军锚点与固定分类器原型对齐，锁定特征空间方向，保持表征拓扑结构，防止适配器漂移。

Result: 采用冻结ResNet-18骨干和可学习弹性适配器实现MAGIC。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200和ImageNet-R上，在1%、5%、10%标签比例下，MAGIC的平均增量准确率超过大多数配备FixMatch的监督CIL方法和原生SSCIL基线。最大增益出现在细粒度、低标签场景中，这正是置信度阈值方法失效最明显的场景。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过几何约束与特征校准有效解决了无范例SSCIL中的特征漂移和伪标签退化问题，无需存储旧数据即可保持特征空间稳定，尤其适合细粒度低标签场景，为半监督类别增量学习提供了一种可行的无样例记忆方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised Class Incremental Learning (SSCIL) is a severe challenge for neural networks, and it is hardest in the exemplar-free setting where no past data may be stored. Existing methods forget catastrophically due to feature drift, and their pseudo-labels become increasingly unreliable as the label space grows. In this paper, we propose MAGIC (Manifold Anchoring and Geometric Incremental Calibration), a framework that stabilizes plasticity without storing exemplars. MAGIC's design centers on two components. The first is Soft-Weighted Geometry Calibration (SWGC), which uses graph-based label propagation on the learner's plastic feature space to weight and calibrate class means and variances computed on the frozen backbone; from these calibrated Gaussians, we sample phantom features that stand in for data from previous tasks. The second is a Geometric Structural Alignment (GSA) objective that preserves representation topology by matching the relational structure of student and teacher heads and aligning feature anchors with the fixed classifier prototypes, locking the orientation of the feature space. Together, these constraints keep the adapter from drifting, so geometric relations between classes remain stable as new classes arrive. We implement MAGIC with a frozen ResNet-18 backbone and a learnable plastic adapter. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, and ImageNet-R, at label ratios of 1%, 5%, and 10%, MAGIC improves average incremental accuracy over most of the supervised CIL methods equipped with FixMatch and native SSCIL baselines; the largest gains occur in the fine-grained, low-label setting, where confidence thresholding fails most clearly.

</details>


### [32] [NewtonGS: Physics-Structured Object-Level Neural Newtonian Dynamics for Gaussian Scene Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07598)
*Lianlei Shan,Feiyang Ye,Yan Chen,Yong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NewtonGS是一个物理结构化框架，用于在静态3D高斯场景中实现物体级别的状态预测和动画生成。每个物体用22维状态表示（包括姿态、线速度、角速度、各向异性缩放及其变化率、质量和接触属性），通过高斯神经牛顿动力学模型结合解析动力学与学习到的残差，实现物体状态滚动和场景动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态高斯方法主要聚焦于重建时变场景或模拟形变，而不是提供紧凑的物体级动态状态用于直接控制。为了填补这一空白，作者提出NewtonGS，旨在为物体提供明确、可控的动态状态表示，从而实现对3D高斯场景中物体的动画控制。

Method: NewtonGS包含三个核心组件：(1) 每个物体用22维状态表示（姿态、线速度、角速度、各向异性缩放及其变化率、质量、接触属性）；(2) 高斯神经牛顿动力学（Gaussian-NND）模型，结合解析的平移、四元数运动学、重力、阻尼和缩放恢复动力学与学习得到的连续和接触残差；(3) 离散事件图处理地面接触。预测的位姿和缩放定义共享仿射变换，更新每个物体关联的所有高斯的均值和协方差。作者还构建了两个程序生成数据集：State-32（状态滚动评估）和Gaussian-32（状态到高斯变换评估）。

Result: 在State-32数据集的分布内和速度范围偏移两种划分上，NewtonGS在轨迹RMSE、最终位移误差和速度RMSE上均优于五个解析基线。在Gaussian-32上的实验进一步证明了从预测状态到动画高斯物体的有效转换。

Conclusion: NewtonGS通过物理结构化框架成功实现了物体级别的状态预测与3D高斯场景动画，在轨迹精度和状态转换效果上优于现有解析基线，为可控制的3D高斯物体动画提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Animating objects in a static 3D Gaussian scene requires an explicit object-level dynamic state and a controllable model of object motion. Existing dynamic Gaussian methods primarily reconstruct time-varying scenes or simulate deformation, rather than provide compact object states for direct control. To address this gap, we present NewtonGS, a physics-structured framework for object-level state rollout and Gaussian scene animation. NewtonGS represents each object with a 22-dimensional state covering pose, linear and angular velocity, anisotropic scale and its rate, mass, and contact properties. Its Gaussian Neural Newtonian Dynamics (Gaussian-NND) model combines analytic translation, quaternion kinematics, gravity, damping, and scale-restoration dynamics with learned continuous and contact residuals. A discrete event map handles floor contact. Predicted poses and scales define a shared affine transformation that updates the means and covariances of all Gaussians associated with each object. We construct two procedurally generated datasets: State-32 for state-rollout evaluation and Gaussian-32 for state-to-Gaussian transformation. On both the in-distribution and velocity-range-shift splits of State-32, NewtonGS achieves lower trajectory RMSE, final displacement error, and velocity RMSE than five analytic baselines. Experiments on Gaussian-32 further demonstrate effective conversion from predicted states to animated Gaussian objects.

</details>


### [33] [HSMLA: Hierarchical Softmax Multi-scale Linear Attention for Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07616)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Renata Borovica-Gajic,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HSMLA（层次化Softmax多尺度线性注意力），通过ReLU线性注意力捕获全局上下文、选择性Softmax细化关键局部特征、深度卷积构建多尺度令牌表示，在密集预测任务上实现最高4.2倍推理加速，同时保持甚至提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer在高分辨率密集预测中因自注意力的二次复杂度面临巨大计算开销；线性注意力虽高效但牺牲了局部上下文建模能力。需要一种既能保持高效又兼顾局部细节的注意力机制。

Method: HSMLA结合三种策略：1) ReLU-based线性注意力用于全局上下文建模；2) 选择性Softmax细化关键局部特征；3) 通过深度卷积生成多尺度令牌表示。三者协同实现精度与效率的平衡。

Result: 在密集预测任务上实现最高4.2倍推理加速；CT器官分割达到87.3% Dice且3.2倍加速；病理WSI分析达到94.2% AUC且4.1倍加速。

Conclusion: HSMLA在精度-效率权衡上优于现有方法，证明了混合线性注意力与Softmax细化机制在密集预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision transformers face significant computational overheads in high-resolution dense prediction due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Linear attention offers efficiency but sacrifices local context modeling. We propose \textbf{HSMLA (Hierarchical Softmax Multi-scale Linear Attention)}, which combines ReLU-based linear attention for global context, selective softmax refinement for critical local features, and multi-scale token representations via depthwise convolutions. HSMLA achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs: up to $4.2\times$ inference-time speedup across dense prediction tasks, $87.3%$ Dice with $3.2\times$ speedup on CT organ segmentation, and $94.2%$ AUC with $4.1\times$ speedup on pathology WSI.

</details>


### [34] [FlowErase-OPD: Multi-Concept Erasure via Anchored On-Policy Distillation in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07620)
*Yi Sun,Yimin Zhou,Xinhao Zhong,Zhiqi Zhang,Junhao Li,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlowErase-OPD框架，基于在线策略蒸馏（OPD）实现流匹配模型中的多概念擦除，通过锚定多教师蒸馏和自适应保留控制，在擦除效果、图像质量和语义对齐之间取得最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在文生图方面取得显著进展，但也带来了生成有害或不当内容的安全隐患。现有概念擦除方法主要针对单一概念，难以有效同时擦除多个概念，因此需要一种能够协调多个擦除目标、同时保留模型生成能力的多概念擦除框架。

Method: FlowErase-OPD包含两个核心组件：(1) Anchored Multi-Teacher Distillation (AMTD)——先将多个单概念擦除模型蒸馏到统一LoRA模块，并引入保留教师以缓解概念擦除与生成能力保留之间的权衡；(2) Adaptive Retention Control (ARC)——在训练过程中动态调整各擦除教师的采样频率和损失权重，以及擦除教师与保留教师之间的相对贡献，以改善多擦除目标的协同性。

Result: 在裸露、物体和艺术风格擦除等多种场景下的大量实验表明，FlowErase-OPD在擦除有效性、图像质量和语义对齐之间持续改善权衡，在多种多概念擦除设置中达到最先进水平。此外，所得模型对对抗性攻击表现出强大的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在线策略蒸馏作为流匹配模型中安全可控生成的通用框架的潜力，为解决多概念擦除中擦除与保留能力之间的权衡问题提供了有效的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have substantially improved the quality of text-to-image generation, but have also raised increasing safety concerns due to their potential to generate harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods for flow matching models predominantly focus on removing individual concepts, while effectively erasing multiple concepts simultaneously remains challenging. We propose FlowErase-OPD, a framework for multi-concept erasure based on on-policy distillation (OPD). Our approach first distills multiple single-concept erased models into a unified LoRA module and introduces Anchored Multi-Teacher Distillation (AMTD), which incorporates a retention teacher to mitigate the trade-off between concept erasure and preservation of generative capabilities. To further improve the coordination of multiple erasure objectives, we develop Adaptive Retention Control (ARC), which dynamically adjusts the sampling frequency and loss weight of each erasure teacher, together with the relative contribution of erasure and retention teachers throughout training. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic-style erasure demonstrate that FlowErase-OPD consistently improves the trade-off between erasure effectiveness, image quality, and semantic alignment, achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse multi-concept erasure settings. Furthermore, the resulting models exhibit strong robustness against adversarial attacks. These results highlight the potential of on-policy distillation as a principled framework for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

</details>

*Jesus Guerrero,Isaac Corley,Leon Najafirad,Maryam Tabar,Paul Rad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HeatCast，一个基于Landsat的月度LST预测基准，覆盖2013年至2025年6月美国124个城市，提供30米分辨率的LST、高程、地表反射率RGB、光谱指数、宽带反照率、质量掩膜和局地气候区标签，并附带固定时间划分、LCZ分层指标及参考评估框架。实验表明Earthformer在逐月预测中达到7.74K RMSE，优于CNN+LSTM的10.42K；仅使用八个非LST通道预测可达7.72K，优于仅用LST历史（8.15K）和RGB（8.68K）。数据、代码和权重已在MIT许可证下公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏共享的30米分辨率地表温度预测基准，以往研究通常只覆盖一到三个城市、使用公里级产品或不公开数据与代码，阻碍了该领域的可复现性和横向比较。

Method: 构建HeatCast基准，包含124个美国城市、30米分辨率的月度LST瓦片及多种辅助地理变量；设定固定时间划分和LCZ分层评估指标；以CNN+LSTM和Earthformer作为基线模型，在次月预测任务上进行评估。

Result: Earthformer达到7.74K RMSE，优于CNN+LSTM的10.42K；仅用八个非LST通道预测为7.72K，优于LST历史（8.15K）和RGB（8.68K）。

Conclusion: HeatCast为30米LST预测提供了首个大规模开放基准，实验表明非LST辅助通道在预测中起关键作用，Earthformer在该任务上表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a widely used satellite-derived measure of urban surface heat, but there is no shared benchmark for forecasting it at 30 m. Prior studies usually cover one to three cities, use kilometer-scale products, or do not release data and code. We introduce HeatCast, a Landsat-based benchmark for monthly LST forecasting across 124 U.S. cities from 2013 through June 2025. HeatCast contains 30 m monthly tiles with LST, elevation, surfacereflectance RGB, three spectral indices, broadband albedo, quality masks, and Local Climate Zone (LCZ) labels, together with a fixed temporal split, LCZ-stratified metrics, and a reference evaluation harness. We evaluate a CNN+LSTM and Earthformer on next-month forecasting, where Earthformer reaches 7.74 K RMSE against 10.42 K for the CNN+LSTM. Forecasting from the eight nonLST channels alone reaches 7.72 K, against 8.15 K from LST history and 8.68 K from RGB. The data, code, and weights are released under MIT at https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/9889.

</details>


### [36] [Data collection from highways: a geometric, class-agnostic approach to embedded vehicle counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07643)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,William W. M. Lira,Alexandre M. Lima,Thales M. A. Vieira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了一种纯几何的交通流量检测流程，其不依赖深度目标检测器或训练集，而是通过背景减除和阈值化实现类别无关的运动目标检测，并在线路穿越计数规则下工作。该系统在树莓派等单板计算机上以超过实时速度运行，无需对象模型或轨迹。两种计数规则分别获得约86%的解析精度和83.3%-100%的实测精度，现场部署达到91%的准确率，远超同等算力下基于blob跟踪的基线（37.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通数据采集依赖深度目标检测器加跟踪的流水线，但这一流水线预设了一个前提：检测器必须已在目标类别上训练好——而实践中这一前提常常不成立。作者因此希望探索一种不依赖目标模型、无需训练数据的纯几何感知方案，以解决开放类别、低算力、隐私受限和冷启动等实际场景下的交通计数问题。

Method: 提出了一个运行于单板计算机上的纯几何交通感知流水线：检测是类别无关的，通过背景减除和阈值化识别移动物体；计数由横跨道路的虚拟线上的几何规则决定（软件感应线圈）。给出两种计数规则：（1）恒定平均速度规则，在高斯速度分布假设下解析推导出约86%的期望准确率；（2）自校准预校准规则，从blob统计中恢复车道几何，计算车道占用边缘进行计数，并额外免费获得每车平均速度。

Result: 在四个视频上，自校准规则达到83.3%-100%的计数准确率；现场部署中达到91%，而相同算力预算下的blob跟踪基线仅37.5%。详细记录了部署期间观察到的现象：准确率崩溃的分辨率下限、车辆混叠越过计数线的帧率下限、短 curated 片段与长未受控素材之间的差距，以及Python（易调参，100% CPU）与C++（40% CPU，热稳定）之间的权衡。这些属性源于采样几何而非当时的硬件，仍约束着边缘部署。

Conclusion: 作者论证了基于运动的类别无关检测仍然是以下场景的正确工具：无标注数据的开放类别、严格功耗预算、隐私受限安装，以及通过挖掘训练样本冷启动学习型检测器。

Abstract: Traffic data collection is dominated today by deep object detectors followed by tracking-by-detection, a pipeline that presupposes what is often missing in practice: a detector already trained on the class one wants to count. We revisit a purely geometric traffic-sensing pipeline for Single Board Computers in which detection is class-agnostic: moving objects come from background subtraction and thresholding, and counting is decided by a geometric rule on an imaginary line across the road, a software inductive loop detector. With no object model, training set or per-object trajectory, it runs faster than real time on Raspberry Pi class hardware. Two counting rules are described: a constant average speed rule, whose expected accuracy is derived analytically as about 86% under a Gaussian speed distribution, and a self-calibrating pre-calibration rule that recovers the lane geometry from blob statistics and counts edges of lane occupancy, additionally yielding per-vehicle average speed at no extra cost. Over four videos the latter counts with 83.3%-100% accuracy; in a field deployment it reaches 91% against 37.5% for a blob-tracking baseline under the same compute budget. We report the observations of that period in detail: the resolution floor below which accuracy collapses, the frame rate floor at which vehicles alias past the counting line, the gap between short curated clips and long uncontrolled footage, and the trade-off between Python (easier to tune, 100% CPU) and C++ (40% CPU, thermally viable). These are properties of the sampling geometry, not of the hardware of the time, and still constrain edge deployments. We close by arguing where motion-based, class-agnostic detection remains the right tool: open-set classes with no annotated data, tight power budgets, privacy-constrained installations, and the cold start of mining training crops to bootstrap a learned detector.

</details>


### [37] [Keep It Simple: Multi-Key Episodic Memory Retrieval for Ultra-Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07663)
*Yeeun Choi,Youngbeom Yoo,Joon-Young Lee,Hyolim Kang,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MERIT是一个面向超长视频理解的智能体框架，通过多键情景检索和推理时的时间扩展，取代传统复杂的记忆构建过程，在三个长视频基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当视频从数小时延伸到数天时，当前的MLLM无法端到端直接处理。现有工作投入大量算力在构建时预建模视频的高层关系，但构建时并不知道下游查询内容，这种"盲目"的记忆构建效率低下。作者认为应在记忆构建阶段优先保证高召回的可检索性，而将查询特定的高层关系组合推迟到推理阶段完成。

Method: MERIT采用两阶段智能体框架：(1) 情景化的多键表示（episodic multi-key representation），通过简单的键匹配机制实现细粒度记忆的精确检索；(2) 邻居过滤机制（neighbor filtering mechanism），在推理时仅围绕检索到的片段扩展时间范围，获取更广泛的语义上下文，避免全局记忆构建的巨大计算开销。

Result: MERIT在三个长视频基准上达到最先进性能：EgoLifeQA、LVBench和Video-MME (Long)。

Conclusion: 通过简单的键匹配配合按需时间扩展，MERIT证明了"简化记忆构建+推理时上下文扩展"这一范式在超长视频理解中的有效性，避免了复杂全局关系预建模的高昂成本。

Abstract: When videos extend from hours to days, directly processing them end-to-end becomes impractical for current Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs). This ultra-long setting necessitates a two-stage paradigm: query-agnostic memory construction followed by retrieval-based inference. Prior work invests in complex memory construction to pre-model high-level relations in videos, despite not knowing the downstream query at build time. We instead prioritize high-recall retrievability during memory building, and defer query-specific, high-level relation composition to inference time. To this end, we propose MERIT(Multi-key Episodic Retrieval with Inference-time Temporal expansion), a simple yet effective agentic framework for ultra-long video understanding. First, we formulate an episodic multi-key representation that enables precise retrieval of fine-grained memories through a simple key-matching mechanism. Second, we introduce a neighbor filtering mechanism to capture broader semantic context without the massive computational overhead of global memory construction. This is achieved by expanding the temporal scope exclusively around the retrieved segments at inference time. By leveraging simple key-matching with this on-demand temporal expansion, MERIT achieves state-of-the-art performance across three long-video benchmarks: EgoLifeQA, LVBench, and Video-MME (Long).

</details>


### [38] [CosmosAlign: Adapting a World Foundation Model for Generative Traffic Video Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07693)
*Quang Minh Dinh,Tuan Kiet Doan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CosmosAlign是一个基于预训练Cosmos3-Nano世界基础模型的生成式交通视频预测框架，通过两阶段LoRA适配（先对齐条件生成分布，再通过LLM重标注对齐训练文本与模型原生提示接口）和推理阶段的无训练优化（共识中位数样本选择与运动自适应静态区域混合），在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5上以76.49分排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域适配与分布对齐（而非增加模型容量）是成功将大型预训练世界模型应用于下游预测任务的关键。现有方法未充分利用预训练模型的潜力，需要一种高效的适配策略来解决观测历史条件分布与预测任务分布之间的差异，以及训练文本与模型原生提示结构之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出两阶段LoRA适配策略：(1)第一阶段对齐条件生成模式与目标预测任务的分布；(2)第二阶段通过基于LLM的重标注流程，将训练描述对齐到模型原生的结构化提示接口。推理阶段采用全免训练流程，包括基于共识的中位数样本选择和运动自适应静态场景区域混合。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5基准上取得76.49的最终分数，在最终排行榜上排名第一。

Conclusion: 验证据了分布对齐而非模型扩容是适配预训练世界模型的关键，两阶段LoRA与免训练推理优化相结合可有效提升生成式交通视频预测性能。

Abstract: Generative traffic video forecasting aims to synthesize long-horizon, temporally coherent future videos of traffic scenes from a short observation history and textual descriptions. In this paper, we present CosmosAlign, a generative traffic video forecasting framework built upon the pretrained Cosmos3-Nano world foundation model. Our approach is motivated by the observation that successfully adapting large pretrained world models to downstream forecasting tasks depends primarily on distribution alignment rather than increased model capacity. To this end, we propose a two-stage LoRA adaptation strategy that first aligns the conditioning-mode distribution with the target forecasting task, and then aligns the training captions with the model's native structured prompting interface through an LLM-based re-captioning pipeline. During inference, we further improve prediction quality using a fully training-free procedure consisting of consensus-based medoid sample selection and motion-adaptive blending of static scene regions. CosmosAlign achieves a final score of 76.49 on the AI City Challenge 2026 Track 5 benchmark, ranking first on the final leaderboard. Our code is publicly available at https://quangminhdinh.github.io/CosmosAlign/.

</details>


### [39] [SpikeWorld: Fast-State Adaptation for Frozen Spiking World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07712)
*Ziqiao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikeWorld，一个145万参数的稀疏脉冲模型，联合训练异构感官预测、语义、图像-文本绑定和动作条件动力学。部署时所有参数冻结，通过延迟下一状态残差更新两条外部路径（累积固定库损失选择有界动作修正，路径特定残差矩阵细化下一状态预测），无需标签、教师输出、奖励或真实位移值。联合优化使动作下一状态MSE提升17.10%，在多种任务上均有改善，且在Meta-World六臂研究（450条轨迹）中将冻结策略奖励提升7.90分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型在观察到动作后果时收到自监督信号，但部署后利用该信号存在困难：当动力学和语义共享参数时，冻结参数阻止适应，而权重更新需要优化器状态且可能改变已学习的表示。因此需要一种无需重新训练即可利用自监督信号进行在线适配的方法。

Method: SpikeWorld采用稀疏脉冲网络，联合训练异构感官预测、语义、图像-文本绑定和动作条件动力学。部署时全部参数冻结，通过两条外部路径进行适配：(1) 累积固定库（fixed-bank）损失选择有界动作修正；(2) 路径特定残差矩阵细化下一状态预测。两条路径均不使用标签、教师输出、奖励、成功信号或真实位移值。

Result: 联合优化使动作下一状态MSE提升17.10%，同时改善多模态预测、语义准确率和图像-文本检索。在保留的剪切和衰减流上，组合外部状态使聚合预测提升5.48%和30.01%；固定库动作路径使跟踪提升24.20%和3.94%。在Meta-World六臂研究中（450条轨迹），冻结策略奖励提升7.90（95% CI [2.48, 14.06]），成功差异13.33分（CI [0, 40]）。16字节RLS估计器在线性衰减上取得最高非预言奖励，表明贡献在于与冻结多模态脉冲检查点的集成而非线性辨识本身。

Conclusion: SpikeWorld证明，在不更新训练参数的情况下，部署期可通过外部残差路径利用自监督信号实现有效适配。对于相同感官输入，模型参数和继承的语义输出保持逐位不变，表明冻结点可作为稳定的基础，同时通过外部可更新机制获得适应性。

Abstract: A predictive model receives a self-supervised signal whenever the consequence of an action is observed. Using that signal after deployment is difficult when dynamics and semantics share parameters: freezing prevents adaptation, whereas weight updates require optimizer state and may alter the learned representation. Here we introduce SpikeWorld, a 1.45M-parameter sparse spiking model jointly trained for heterogeneous sensory prediction, semantics, image-text binding and action-conditioned dynamics. At deployment, all trained parameters are frozen. Delayed next-state residuals update two external paths: cumulative fixed-bank losses select the bounded action correction, while route-specific residual matrices refine next-state prediction. Neither path uses labels, teacher outputs, rewards, success signals or the true shift value. Joint optimization improves action next-state MSE by 17.10\% while also improving multimodal prediction, semantic accuracy and image-text retrieval. On held-out shear and attenuation streams, the combined external state improves aggregate prediction by 5.48\% and 30.01\%; its fixed-bank action path improves tracking by 24.20\% and 3.94\%, respectively. In a six-arm study comprising 450 new Meta-World trajectories (75 per arm), SpikeWorld raises frozen-policy reward by 7.90 (95\% CI [2.48, 14.06]); the 13.33-point success difference is descriptive (CI [0, 40]). For identical sensory inputs, model parameters and inherited semantic outputs remain bitwise unchanged. A 16-byte RLS estimator obtains the highest non-oracle reward on linear attenuation, showing that the contribution is not superior linear identification, but its integration with a frozen multimodal spiking checkpoint. Reference code is publicly available at https://github.com/Oooorca/SpikeWorld.

</details>


### [40] [Tokenizer Generator Coupling in Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07713)
*Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在受控的ChestMNIST 64x64设置下，系统检验了潜在医学图像生成器中"tokenizer固定预处理"这一假设，发现生成器排名取决于tokenizer、生成器与采样器的三方交互；并提出生成器无关统计量"邻域条件预测增益"以分离量化器族的生成质量（rank-AUC 1.00），优于重建PSNR等传统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在医学图像生成器通常将tokenizer视为固定预处理步骤而忽略其与生成器的交互影响。作者质疑这一分离假设的有效性，希望在受控条件下验证tokenizer、生成器、采样器之间的相互依赖关系是否成立。

Method: 在ChestMNIST 64x64受控研究中，交叉离散tokenizer、生成器族和采样器设置于共享潜在网格，并以连续潜在参考单元对照。词汇量1024交互块在三个种子下重训练验证交互显著性；引入生成器无关统计量"邻域条件预测增益"（neighbour-conditional predictive gain）评估量化器族；对LFQ-1024上的D3PM和SE-D3PM在验证集上重新调参。采用率失真-可建模性（rate-distortion-modelability）框架解释结果。

Result: 最佳量化器随生成器而改变，验证集采样器选择会改变生成器排名；交互效应在6/9的成对量化器比较中超过三个种子标准差。重建PSNR不能作为可靠选择标准，而新提出的邻域条件预测增益以rank-AUC 1.00分离量化器族。对LFQ-1024重调参后D3PM/SE-D3PM从FID-192 0.44/0.41降至0.09/0.10且跨种子复现。FID-192与FID-2048（Spearman 0.80）及无标签分类器双样本检验（0.78）一致。

Conclusion: tokenizer与生成器/采样器之间的交互在受控设置下真实存在且显著，不应将tokenizer视为与生成过程无关的固定预处理；重建质量指标不足以指导量化器选择，模型可生成性（modelability）取决于生成器、采样器与推理预算的共同作用。所有结论限定于64x64低分辨率医学风格图像的无条件生成场景。

Abstract: Latent medical image generators usually treat the tokenizer as fixed preprocessing. We test whether this separation is valid in a controlled ChestMNIST study at 64x64, crossing discrete tokenizers, generator families, and sampler settings under a shared latent grid, with continuous-latent reference cells. In this controlled setting, rankings depend jointly on the tokenizer, generator, and sampler: the best quantizer changes with the generator, and validation-based sampler selection changes the apparent generator ranking. We retrain the vocabulary-1024 interaction block at three seeds and the interaction survives (6 of 9 pairwise quantizer comparisons exceed three seed standard deviations), and we scope the wider single-seed grid accordingly. Reconstruction PSNR alone is not a reliable selection criterion; we instead introduce a generator-free statistic, neighbour-conditional predictive gain, that separates the quantizer families by downstream generation quality (rank-AUC 1.00) where reconstruction PSNR and marginal token entropy do not. On LFQ-1024, retuning D3PM and SE-D3PM (selected on a held-out validation split) moves them from default FID-192 0.44/0.41 to 0.09/0.10 at lower NFE, replicated across seeds; the continuous references were not given an equivalent sampler sweep. We report FID-192 as an internal ranking metric; it ranks consistently with standard FID-2048 (Spearman 0.80) and with a label-free classifier two-sample test (0.78). We interpret these results through a rate-distortion-modelability framing, where modelability is conditional on the generator, sampler, and inference budget. All experiments are at 64x64 on low-resolution medical-style images, unconditional, and evaluated with non-clinical FID-based metrics, and we scope every claim to that setting. Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViWi框架，通过材料槽（material slots）聚合共享材料身份的体素证据，并结合WiFi频段电磁仿真生成的射频描述符来补充视觉信息，实现更准确且物理一致的体积力学属性估计。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: ViWi采用物体中心框架，核心包括：(1) 材料槽表示——用紧凑的材料槽集合表示每个物体，聚合共享材料身份的体素证据，生成槽级一致的属性预测；(2) 射频描述符——通过WiFi频段电磁仿真（利用介电常数和电导率）生成紧凑RF描述符，为材料槽提供图像中缺失的全局组成线索；(3) 视觉特征保留体素级空间定位信息，与RF线索互补。

Result: 在体积力学属性和质量估计基准上，ViWi在六个逐体素指标中的四个上优于先前最先进方法；其纯视觉变体在所有质量估计指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: 将物体中心的材料结构与互补的射频证据相结合，能够实现比仅依赖视觉外观更准确且物理上更一致的体积属性估计。

Abstract: Estimating volumetric mechanical properties, including Young's modulus, Poisson's ratio, and density at each voxel, is intrinsically ambiguous from vision alone, as visually similar objects may have substantially different material compositions and physical behavior. Existing approaches predict these properties independently across voxels, overlooking the piecewise-constant material structure of real objects and producing noisy or inconsistent estimates for voxels that share the same material, while lacking an explicit mechanism to resolve visual ambiguity. We introduce ViWi (Vision Meets WiFi), an object-centric framework for volumetric mechanical-property estimation. ViWi represents each object using a compact set of material slots that aggregate evidence from voxels with a shared material identity and produce coherent slot-level property predictions. To complement visual appearance, ViWi incorporates a compact RF descriptor generated through WiFi-band electromagnetic simulation using permittivity and conductivity. The RF descriptor conditions the material slots with global composition cues that may be unavailable from images, while visual features preserve voxel-level spatial localization. Across volumetric mechanical-property and mass-estimation benchmarks, ViWi improves over the prior state of the art on four of six per-voxel metrics, while its vision-only variant improves all mass-estimation metrics. These results demonstrate that combining object-centric material structure with complementary RF evidence enables more accurate and physically coherent volumetric property estimation beyond what is possible from visual appearance alone.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 通过超复数虚部生成的鬼特征包含增强信息，结合幽灵迁移学习能够有效利用这些特征，构建更高效的神经网络。

Conclusion: 超复数神经网络层与幽灵迁移学习为提升神经网络效率提供了新途径，代码与示例已开源供复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: Hypercomplex numbers extend the concept of complex numbers by introducing additional imaginary components. Besides increasing dimensionality, operations on the imaginary parts provide algebraic and geometrical properties that can be beneficial for solving machine learning problems. In this paper, we show how to create hypercomplex-valued neural network layers where the real part corresponds to the output of a traditional real-valued layer. The additional imaginary parts of these hypercomplex-valued layers produce what we call ``ghost features,'' which contain enhanced information that is not present in the output of the real-valued layer. Moreover, ghost features can be effectively integrated into a trained neural network through a process we refer to as ``spooky transfer learning.'' This approach allows us to harness the richness of ghost features, leading to more efficient neural networks. The source code and Jupyter Notebook are available at https://github.com/mevalle/v-nets/.

</details>


### [43] [BRUCE: Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation for Scientific Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07742)
*Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BRUCE框架，用于系统评估视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在不同图像扰动和失真条件下的多模态推理鲁棒性，并引入RCI和T-RCI两个新指标量化推理性能随腐败严重程度恶化的速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架主要关注干净任务上的准确性，很少分析VLMs的推理稳定性如何在各种鲁棒性维度上退化。实际应用中输入图像质量往往较低或变化较大，VLMs因此面临严重的鲁棒性问题。

Method: BRUCE框架包含两部分：1）对输入图像施加广泛的扰动（如模糊、低对比度等），并在多个严重度级别下进行渐进式扰动缩放；2）提出两个新指标——Robustness Corruption Index (RCI)和Traversal-RCI (T-RCI)——用于量化VLMs多模态推理性能随视觉腐败严重度增加而退化的速率。

Result: 在化学和数学推理任务上对多个数据集进行了评估，并将腐败引起的预测失败归类为四个高层推理域：OCR依赖推理、空间推理、符号推理和语义失败，每个域包含细粒度的腐败特化失败子类型，从而实现可解释的失败分析。

Conclusion: BRUCE提供了一个系统的多模态推理脆弱性基准，能够揭示VLMs在视觉腐败下的推理退化模式，并通过细粒度的失败分类支持可解释的鲁棒性分析。

Abstract: Visual-language models (VLMs) frequently struggle with robustness issues in real-world situations due to low- or varying-quality input images. In this paper, we aim at analyzing VLMs' robustness by applying perturbations and distortions to the input images, such as blur or low contrast. Toward this goal, we propose BRUCE (Benchmarking Robustness Under Corruption Escalation, a multimodal reasoning fragility framework for scientific vision-language reasoning.
  State-of-the-art evaluation frameworks/studies primarily focus on clean-task accuracy and rarely analyze how reasoning stability degrades across robustness dimensions. Besides varying over a wide-range of input perturbations, BRUCE employs two novel metrics -- Robustness Corruption Index (RCI) and Traversal-RCI (T-RCI) -- to quantify how rapidly multimodal reasoning performance deteriorates in VLMs as visual corruption severity increases under progressive perturbation scaling. We evaluate BRUCE across chemistry and mathematical reasoning tasks for multiple datasets, while analyzing corruption-induced prediction failures in terms of four high-level reasoning domains: OCR-dependent reasoning, spatial reasoning, symbolic reasoning, and semantic failures, with each containing fine-grained corruption specific failure subtypes, thereby enabling an interpretable failure analysis.

</details>


### [44] [LoRSA: Toward Generalizable Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Biomedical Downstream Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07749)
*Saed Moradi,Benyamin Ghojogh,M. Hadi Sepanj,Yimin Yang,Ashirbani Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRSA（全局-残差适配框架），通过联合学习一个稠密低秩组件和一个动态结构化稀疏低秩组件，解决参数高效微调中单低秩更新限制模型泛化能力的问题。在乳腺密度分类任务上，LoRSA在保持内部验证集竞争力的同时，在两个外部未见域数据集上分别超越最强基线2.15和3.09个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽然能在有限计算资源下将视觉基础模型适配到生物医学任务，但单个低秩更新将所有任务特定变化限制在一个狭窄的参数字空间中，无法同时表达全局共享的任务结构和局部残差方向，从而限制了模型对未见成像域的泛化能力。

Method: LoRSA框架包含两个互补组件：（1）稠密低秩组件，用于捕获全局协调的任务适配；（2）动态结构化稀疏低秩组件，其支撑集在训练过程中演化，提供互补的残差修正。论文还对该分解的表示容量、逼近性质、秩结构和奇异子空间互补性进行了理论刻画。

Result: 在DINOv3-Base上评估四类乳腺密度分类，以VinDr-Mammo为源域，MammosighTR和RSNA为外部未见域。LoRSA在内部验证集上保持竞争力，在MammosighTR上超越最强基线2.15个百分点，在RSNA上超越3.09个百分点。权重矩阵分析显示约92%的每个适配组件能量位于另一组件的双侧奇异子空间之外，表明两个组件学习到高度互补的更新方向。

Conclusion: 将适配容量组织为不同的全局路径和残差路径可以提升参数高效适配生物医学视觉模型的外部域泛化能力，为参数高效微调的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables the adaptation of vision foundation models to biomedical tasks under limited computational resources, but a single low-rank update can constrain all task-specific changes to one narrow parameter subspace. This restriction may prevent the model from simultaneously representing globally shared task structure and localized residual directions required for generalization to unseen imaging domains. We introduce LoRSA, a global--residual adaptation framework that jointly learns a dense low-rank component and a dynamically structured-sparse low-rank component. The dense component captures globally coordinated task adaptation, while the structured component provides complementary residual corrections whose support evolves during training. We characterize the representational capacity, approximation properties, rank structure, and singular-subspace complementarity of this decomposition. We evaluate LoRSA for four-class breast-density classification using DINOv3-Base, with VinDr-Mammo as the source domain and MammosighTR and RSNA as unseen external domains. LoRSA remains competitive on the internal validation set and achieves the best external macro-F1 on both target datasets, improving upon the strongest competing method by 2.15 percentage points on MammosighTR and 3.09 percentage points on RSNA. Weight-matrix analysis further shows that approximately $92\%$ of the energy of each adaptation component lies outside the bilateral singular subspace of the other, indicating that the two components learn largely complementary update directions. These results suggest that organizing adaptation capacity into distinct global and residual paths can improve the external-domain generalization of parameter-efficiently adapted biomedical vision models.

</details>


### [45] [Multi-Task Consistency-based Detection of Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07750)
*Cong Chen,Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用多任务感知中任务间推理结果不一致性来检测对抗攻击的高效方案，在BDD100k验证集上对PGD攻击实现了99.9%的ROC-AUC检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在视觉感知系统中广泛应用，但易受对抗攻击影响，尤其在自动驾驶场景中威胁显著。现有防御方法普遍存在成本高、效率低的问题，难以在资源受限的系统中实际部署。

Method: 利用复杂视觉系统中的多任务感知特性，通过检测多个视觉任务（如目标检测和实例分割）推理输出之间的不一致性来识别对抗扰动。具体包括：(1) 设计一致性评分指标衡量任务间输出不一致程度；(2) 设计模型配对选择方法以有效检测不一致性；(3) 在BDD100k验证集上对PGD攻击进行评估。

Result: 在考虑的攻撃模型下，所提出的防御方法在BDD100k验证集上实现了99.9%的ROC-AUC检测性能。

Conclusion: 该方案通过利用多任务感知的内在一致性作为自然防线，在高效检测对抗攻击方面表现出色，为资源受限场景下的对抗防御提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have found successful deployment in numerous vision perception systems. However, their susceptibility to adversarial attacks has prompted concerns regarding their practical applications, specifically in the context of autonomous driving. Existing defenses often suffer from cost inefficiency, rendering their deployment impractical for resource-constrained applications. In this work, we propose an efficient and effective adversarial attack detection scheme leveraging the multi-task perception within a complex vision system. Adversarial perturbations are detected by the inconsistencies between the inference outputs of multiple vision tasks, e.g., object detection and instance segmentation. To this end, we developed a consistency score metric to measure the inconsistency between vision tasks. Next, we designed an approach to select the best model pairs for detecting inconsistencies effectively. Finally, we evaluated our defense against PGD attacks across multiple vision models on the BDD100k validation dataset. The experimental results demonstrated that our defense achieved a ROC-AUC performance of 99.9% detection within the considered attacker model.

</details>


### [46] [Rethinking 3D Segmentation from Individual LiDAR Scans: Incidence-Aware Sampling on the SIP Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07757)
*Seongyong Kim,Jingdao Chen,Yong Kwon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对单个LiDAR扫描的入射感知采样策略，用于施工现场3D场景理解，在固定片段点预算下通过几何归一化流形空间进行体素选择，保留了原始欧氏坐标，无需修改骨干网络。在SIP基准上使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt验证，提升了分辨率的平均分割性能，尤其对非平面元素和梯子的分割效果更好，同时降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解方法多基于精心整理的数据集，未能反映真实施工现场的传感条件。实际工作中单个LiDAR扫描只能提供局部更新，存在表面覆盖有限、密度随采集方式变化、平面表面与稀疏施工元素之间严重不平衡等问题。由于大点云必须降采样，采样分辨率和点分配直接影响几何细节与空间上下文之间的平衡，因此需要一种考虑采集条件的主动采样策略。

Method: 提出入射感知采样策略：将点映射到几何归一化的流形空间进行基于体素的选择，同时保留原始欧氏坐标用于下游学习。该方法仅需点坐标和法向量，无需修改骨干网络，在固定每片段点预算下执行。

Result: 在Site in Pieces (SIP)基准上，使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt进行实验，结果显示平均分割性能得到提升，尤其在非平面元素和梯子上的分割效果更好，同时降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。

Conclusion: 采集感知的采样能够提供更稳定的几何表示，应被视为单个扫描3D分割中的主动组成部分，而非通用预处理步骤。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding is increasingly important in construction, yet most methods are developed on curated datasets that do not fully reflect real site sensing conditions. In many workflows, individual LiDAR scans provide rapid local updates rather than complete scene representations, producing limited surface coverage, acquisition-driven density variation, and severe imbalance between dominant planar surfaces and sparse construction elements. Because large point clouds must be downsampled, sampling resolution and point allocation directly affect the balance between geometric detail and spatial context. This study evaluates these effects under a fixed per-fragment point budget and introduces an incidence-aware sampling strategy for individual LiDAR scans. The method maps points to a geometry-normalized manifold space for voxel-based selection while preserving original Euclidean coordinates for downstream learning. It requires only point coordinates and normals and no backbone modification. Using the Site in Pieces (SIP) benchmark, experiments with Point Transformer and PointNeXt show improved resolution-averaged segmentation performance, especially for non-planar elements and ladders, while reducing sensitivity to sampling resolution. The results show that acquisition-aware sampling can provide a more stable geometric representation and should be treated as an active component of individual-scan 3D segmentation rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [47] [XClipGS: Exact Half-Space Clipping for Medical Volume Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07760)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XClipGS提出一种精确的3D高斯溅射剪裁方法，通过EWA溅射的局部仿射模型将半空间限制高斯射线的积分精确分解为普通2D足迹与条件高斯CDF的闭式乘积，实现无需学习参数的可微剪裁算子，并用多距离参考视图监督内部结构，在多个CT/MRI体数据上取得最佳PSNR且渲染速度超650 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯溅射代理虽能实现医学体数据的交互式渲染，但剪裁平面会暴露未受外部视角训练约束的解剖结构，且与图元相交时传统溅射只能整体保留或丢弃。现有方法缺乏对隐藏内部的监督，且全局图像指标会稀释剪裁面附近的误差，需要更精确的剪裁运算与定向评估协议。

Method: 方法将渲染时剪裁算子和内部监督视为两个独立问题。在EWA溅射的局部仿射模型下，将半空间限制高斯射线的积分精确分解为普通2D足迹与像素坐标仿射参数的条件高斯CDF的闭式乘积，得到无学习参数的逐像素可微算子。使用多距离、多剪裁轴向与偏移的参考视图，通过同一算子监督内部。同时引入配对剪裁/未剪裁切面协议，提出差参考切面误差（CDE）与剔除侧泄漏（Leak）指标，避免全局指标稀释剪裁面附近误差。

Result: 在八个CT/MRI体数据及未用于训练的平面偏移上，XClipGS在全部体数据上取得最高PSNR（33.56 dB对ClipGS的32.34 dB），渲染速度超过650 FPS（对比278 FPS）。在体素轴切面视图上，平均带SSIM从0.809提升至0.860，泄漏约减少40倍。无需重新训练即可在任意法向平面上取得四项指标平均最优；在固定内部上以约16倍少的泄漏匹配RaRa的切面保真度。

Conclusion: XClipGS通过理论精确的闭式剪裁算子与专门的内部监督策略，显著提升医学体数据剪裁渲染的精度与速度，同时配套引入切面针对性评估指标，为高斯溅射在医学可视化的应用提供了更可靠、高效的方案。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting proxies enable interactive rendering of volumetric medical scans, but a clipping plane exposes anatomy not constrained by external-view training and intersects primitives that conventional splatting can only keep or drop whole. We present XClipGS (eXact Clipping), which treats these as two separate problems: the render-time clip operator and supervision of the hidden interior. Under the local affine model used by EWA splatting, the ray integral of a half-space-restricted Gaussian factorizes exactly into its ordinary 2D footprint and a conditional Gaussian CDF whose argument is affine in pixel coordinates. The resulting closed-form per-pixel operator introduces no learned clipping parameters or auxiliary network and remains differentiable with respect to the primitive and plane. We use multi-distance reference views with varied clipping-plane axes and offsets to supervise the interior through the same operator. We also introduce a paired clipped/unclipped cut-face protocol with difference-referenced cut error (CDE) and culled-side leakage (Leak), because global image metrics dilute errors near the plane. On eight CT and MRI volumes with plane offsets not used for training, XClipGS attains the highest PSNR on every volume (33.56 versus 32.34 dB for ClipGS) while rendering at over 650 FPS, far above real time, versus 278 FPS. On voxel-axis cut-face views, it raises average band SSIM from 0.809 to 0.860 and leaks roughly 40 times less. Without retraining, it also achieves the best average across all four metrics on arbitrary-normal planes; on a fixed interior, it matches RaRa's face fidelity with about 16 times less leakage. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/XClipGS/

</details>


### [48] [DINO-3DRA: Leveraging 2D Foundation Model Semantics for 3D Cerebral Aneurysm Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07767)
*Jiayang Lu,Fengming Lin,Alejandro F. Frangi,Ali Sarrami-Foroushani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DINO-3DRA提出一种双路径框架，通过将冻结的2D视觉基础模型（DINOv3）特征注入3D U-Net骨干网络，实现跨维度语义迁移，在3D旋转血管造影动脉瘤分割任务上达到SOTA性能（Dice: 0.758），仅需5.72M可训练参数，且无需微调即可消除所有灾难性失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DRA中的动脉瘤分割面临三大挑战：极端类别不平衡、与血管形态相似性高、缺乏大规模3D预训练。虽然2D视觉基础模型蕴含丰富的稠密结构先验（基于17亿图像），但朴素的逐切片迁移会破坏解剖连续性并导致优化不稳定。因此需要一种有效的跨维度语义迁移方法。

Method: 提出DINO-3DRA双路径框架：将冻结的DINOv3特征通过Room-Lite空间混合模块和校准残差融合机制注入3D U-Net骨干网络，实现跨维度语义迁移。该方法不依赖损失函数设计，而是通过结构化跨维度特征桥接来改善动脉瘤与母血管之间的解剖连续性。

Result: 在多中心3DRA数据上达到SOTA性能：Dice 0.758，HD95 2.75mm，较nnU-Net提升13%；仅需5.72M可训练参数。消融实验证实性能提升来源于结构化跨维度迁移而非损失设计。在不微调CADA和SHINY-ICARUS数据集的情况下，消除了基线架构中所有灾难性失败案例。

Conclusion: DINO-3DRA证明冻结的2D视觉基础模型特征可以通过精心设计的空间混合与残差融合策略有效地注入3D分割框架，为3D医学影像分割提供了一种无需大规模3D预训练的高效解决方案，并在异构成像协议下展现出强大的泛化能力。
